Front pockets: scoop mouths, with bags in cotton twill 170 g/m², natural #D8CFBE, each faced with the denim 10 cm deep so no bag cloth shows at the mouth. Hair elastics: two, fabric-covered in ecru cotton #E2DBCB, no metal. Jacket (end of SC17 only): bone moleskin #D6CCBA. Story-red check (no product): the REC dot #D7261E is approx L*47, C*82, h 37°. The shirt (C*3) and the belt and boots (C*4) clear by chroma; the denim is 223° away. The pistol grip #2B2B2B gives 5.5:1 on the denim.
- **fit notes**: Shirt: shoulder seam at the shoulder point, 12 cm ease at the chest. It is tucked 10 cm deep, so it stays in when she leans across JEAN's knees (V01-B09) and walks the frontage (B10). Unrolled, the sleeves end at the wrist bone. Two 6 cm turns put the roll's lower edge 12 cm above the wrist bone, the L01 cuff landmark, so the right-hand scar, the right-thumb nick and the left-index burn (if kept) read the same at the bar and in the car. The neckline is open down to the third button at the sternum. From the first second of V01-B08, one open arm of PR-04 hooks over the placket edge above that button, lenses outside the shirt, until she unhooks it in SC18. Belt on its middle hole. Jeans front pockets (the same jeans as L01): the scoop mouth runs 11 cm along the waistband and 8 cm down the side seam. The bag is 19 cm deep from the waistband top and 14 cm wide. Its fly-side edge carries on into a stay stitched into the fly, so a load hangs across the front instead of dragging the waistband at the side. Right front, V01-B09 to SC18: PR-01 sits upright, pushed fully in, its top 4 cm below the waistband top, level with the waistband's lower seam. Nothing rises behind the 3 cm belt; the denim shows only a flat 7 cm-wide outline (PR-01 S7). Seated at the reclined wheel (B09), the phone's lower end reaches the hip crease without bending. Seated upright in a chair, the 15 cm phone would bend across the crease, so in SC18 she takes it out standing, before she sits (flagged to the director). Left front: the PR-61 keys lie flat from B09. Right back: PR-02 with 6 cm of grip showing and nothing covering it. The front bag and the back pocket sit on opposite sides of the side seam and never overlap. The belt on its middle hole carries the phone and the pistol without the waistband dipping on the right. Clear zone: no cuff, hair or strap enters the lower-right 15% of the phone frame; only her thumb does.
- **accessories**: Nothing worn. Ears, neck, wrists and both hands are bare: no ring, watch or bracelet. Her hands touch the phone, his collar, his chin and the glovebox (taste note: hands before faces).; Nails bare, short, filed square. The right-hand scar and right-thumb nick stay uncovered, and so does the left-index burn if the director keeps it.; Set-owned: PR-01 in the right front jeans pocket from V01-B09 to SC18; PR-02 in the right back pocket from V01-B09; PR-61 in the left front pocket from V01-B09; PR-04 hooked in her neckline from V01-B08 to SC18.; No sunglasses, hat, headscarf or bag worn.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: medium-deep, warm undertone, #A36A48 approx. Washed: no base, kohl or powder. Pores, freckles and laugh lines show, and her own heat sheen on the forehead and nose is left alone. Brows brushed up with clear gel. Lips: clear balm. Hair: dark brown #3A2417 approx, dense 3B curls with honey highlights #B98A55 approx. It is gathered into one low puff at the nape, about 18 cm across, held with the two ecru elastics, and smoothed back from the hairline along a centre part with gel. The curl texture stays and is never straightened. Nothing hangs loose at the face or neck, so the wind can't blow hair across her hands or the phone (the casting bible leaves this to the stylist). The puff holds unchanged from SC17 through V01 to SC18. Never lightened by makeup or the 35mm look.
- **continuity locks**: [wide: V01 34-42 s and S01] White shirt tucked in all round, pale denim, brown-black belt showing, no jacket (the jacket appears only at the end of SC17): white over pale blue.; [insert, phone-frame edges] Sleeves rolled, lower edge 12 cm above the wrist bone. Both wrists and hands bare.; [medium] Crease state: S1, pressed, through SC17; S2 from V01-B01, two horizontal creases just above the belt and one at each inner elbow.; [close-up] One low puff at the nape, centre part, no loose strands at the face or neck.; [medium, V01-B08 to SC18] PR-04 hooked at the centre of the neckline, above the fastened third button.; [medium back and three-quarter, from V01-B09] Pistol grip 6 cm above the right back pocket, shirt tucked clear of it. The right front shows a flat phone outline, with nothing above the pocket mouth or behind the belt. The left front shows only a flat key outline.

### JEAN_L01 (JEAN)

- **garment silhouette**: Closet: mid-price and bought to be looked at. He spends on the shirt and the shoes and skimps on the trousers and belt, which he hasn't replaced since he got heavier. Story: Thursday night into Friday, early October 2026 (assumed), SC04-SC15. The city is at about 19 °C and the bar and apartment are warm, so he wears one black outfit and no jacket. Head to toe: a black long-sleeve dress shirt with a semi-spread collar on a 4 cm stand, no chest pocket, 7 buttons. The top button is open and the second fastened; cuffs buttoned; tucked in all round. Black flat-front trousers, mid rise, straight leg, half break. A brown-black belt on hole 1, the hole nearest the buckle (the loosest). Black mid-calf socks. Brown-black leather Derbies (PR-41). Plain black boxer briefs. SC15: clothes off (PR-56), bare back and arms, the white sheet at his waist. Older than the story: the belt.
- **materials colors**: Shirt: mercerised cotton twill 150 g/m², matte, blue-black #24262B (approx L*15, C*4, h 260°). Collar band 43 cm, with removable stays. Buttons: matte black corozo #26272B. Trousers: cotton-stretch twill 280 g/m², matte, blue-black #25272C, in the shirt's cast so the two read as one mass (verified: 'The man is dressed in all black, as her black hole'). Boxer briefs: cotton-modal jersey, black #232428, plain waistband, no lettering. Socks: cotton-nylon, black #232427. Belt: 3 cm cowhide, brown-black #2A2624 (approx L*15), 5 holes 2.5 cm apart, buckle in matte black oxide #2B2C2E approx. Older than the story: a deep crease with cracked grain at the middle (third) hole, where it fastened before he got heavier, and the tip (tongue end) worn pale #5A4A40. To rebuild, bend and sand it. Derbies (PR-41): calf leather, brown-black #2A2624, stitched welt, black sole edge, no coloured sole, no logo. This hex replaces the set's #151515. The route's #111111 is the graded read. In a neutral grade the fabric is #24262B, which gives 9.5:1 on the bone headrest #D9CBB0. Nothing branded.
- **fit notes**: 184 cm, US men's L, 36 waist. Shirt: shoulder seam at the shoulder point. With the top button open, the collar stands 1-2 cm clear of his neck. Cuffs at the wrist bone. The shirt is tucked 10 cm deep, and the front blouses 2 cm over the belt where his belly sits over it. The button line pulls across the belly but never gapes: a hidden snap sits between the 4th and 5th buttons. Trousers: waist 3 cm below the navel and snug (he has outgrown them), 20 cm leg opening, half break on the Derbies, with room in the seat to lift and carry VENUS (SC14). The belt is on hole 1, nearest the buckle, 5 cm out from the old crease, which sits just past the buckle. SC10: shoes off on the mat (PR-41 S2), socks on. Builds SH-1 and TR-1 are listed under JEAN_L02.
- **accessories**: PR-04 (set-owned), SC04-SC14: hooked by its left arm in the shirt placket over the fastened second button, a night-time affectation. The left arm goes in behind the button, the right arm is folded, and the frame hangs outside with the lenses across the placket at mid-chest. A clear nylon thread tack behind the second button holds it through the SC09 back seat and the SC14 carry. SC15: VENUS lays it, folded, on top of his folded shirt on PR-55 (PR-56 S2), which is where she takes it from in SC17. The black acetate reads on the black shirt by its gloss, not its colour.; Nothing else worn: no watch, ring, chain or bracelet. Neck and hands bare. His right hand holds his phone and stands the straw.; Set-owned: PR-35 in his right front trouser pocket, no bulge above the pocket line.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: deep, warm undertone, #6E4631 approx at the cheekbone (casting hex). No base. Left uncovered: the pores on the nose and cheeks, the two forehead lines, the crow's feet, the under-eye puffiness from three nights of drinking and the forehead shine. Hair: black #17110E approx, dense tight 4A curls. The top is 12-15 cm stretched, about 5 cm as it sits. It is picked out at the crown with a wide-tooth pick and set with a light leave-in, no gel, so the top shifts as one mass and the crown ends flutter in wind (A16 test). The sides and back are tapered to 1 cm, with no part and no razor line. Squared hairline, the left temple receding further than the right. Beard: close at 3 mm, with a hard razored cheek line and a neckline 2 cm above the Adam's apple; the 3 mm moustache joins the beard. The lentil-sized mole on the left side of the neck stays uncovered. Do not lighten him, de-age him or loosen the curl, in makeup or in the 35mm look.
- **continuity locks**: [wide] All black head to toe, shirt tucked with a 2 cm blouse over the belt, belt showing. No jacket, no jewellery, no watch.; [SC04-SC15 close-up] Top button open, second fastened (the reverse of JEAN_L02's fastened top button). Collar on its stays; mole on the LEFT side of the neck above the collar.; [medium, SC04-SC14] PR-04 hanging from the placket by its left arm over the fastened second button, lenses outside at mid-chest, right arm folded. [SC15 wide] PR-04 folded on top of his folded shirt on the white chair.; [insert, belt] Fastened on hole 1; the cracked crease at the middle hole sits 5 cm past the buckle.; [close-up] Beard 3 mm with a hard cheek line. Top picked out to about 5 cm high as it sits; sides 1 cm.; [SC15 wide] Clothes off, on the floor, then folded on the white chair. Bare back and arms, sheet at the waist.

### JEAN_L02 (JEAN)

- **garment silhouette**: Story: the same shirt, trousers, belt, socks, briefs and Derbies as JEAN_L01; only the production builds differ (see fit_notes). SC16, morning: undressed, face-down under the sheet, right arm off the bed. SC17, the apartment at 21 °C: VENUS dresses him, seats him in the armchair and puts PR-04 on him, taking it from the folded pile. V01, the open car in the desert, 27 °C falling to 17 °C: long sleeves, because she dressed him. SC18: the motel at night. He is dressed her way, not his: every button fastened, including the top one, with no tie. Cuffs buttoned. Tucked flat, with no blouse over the belt. The belt is on hole 1, where he wore it. The fastened top button is the change the camera sees: it sits at the collar her hand straightens in V01-B02, and he would never have done it himself.
- **materials colors**: As JEAN_L01: shirt twill 150 g/m², blue-black #24262B; trousers stretch twill 280 g/m², blue-black #25272C; belt and Derbies brown-black #2A2624; buckle black oxide #2B2C2E approx; socks #232427; briefs #232428. Build hardware: SH-4 snaps in flat black oxide #26272B approx, matte; TR-2 invisible zip, black nylon coil #232428. Fold state from PR-55, pressed into SH-2, SH-3 and SH-4: one horizontal crease across the chest 12 cm below the collar and two vertical creases 10 cm in from the side seams. Desert dust is set-owned PR-57, #C9A45C approx, matte. C1: a film on the shoulders and collar points at V01-B01. C2: her hand brushes the points back to blue-black at B02. C3: a fresh film on the points from B05, glinting under the video light, through B08, B11 and SC18.
- **fit notes**: Builds: all in the L01 fabrics, buttons and stays. He is seated or slumped in every L02 frame. SH-1, the live hero: JEAN_L01 in SC04-SC15, then PR-56 S1-S2 (on the floor, then folded). Its collar band is 43 cm, and its top button is never fastened on screen. SH-2, the dead hero: the collar band is cut 1 cm larger (44 cm), so the fastened top button closes flat on his thick neck with the mole still clear below the ear. It serves the SC17 held frame in the armchair, V01-B01 and B03-B14, V02, S01 and SC18. SH-3, the dust repeat: identical to SH-2 and pre-dusted to C1 by production design (PR-57). It is swapped in for each retake of V01-B02 and V02-B01, so every brush take starts from C1 while SH-2 holds its later state. SH-4, the dressing double: as SH-2, with a centre-back split from the collar band to the hem, closed by 7 flat snaps under a 3 cm underlap and unseen from the front and sides. It is used in SC17 while she dresses him on screen, and for loading him into the car seat and the motel chair. It is never used in V01 0-56 s, where the phone sees the back of his collar. TR-1, the hero trousers: JEAN_L01, SC04-SC15, including the SC14 carry. TR-2, the dressing double: an invisible zip in the right side seam from waistband to hem, the same waist as TR-1 and 2 cm more seat ease, so the seat seam doesn't strain as he slumps. It is used in every L02 frame; in V01 the right seam lies against the passenger door. Belts x2: the hero and a matched copy, with the crease and pale tip rebuilt the same way. Derbies: the hero pair, plus a pair half a size up with the laces pre-set loose, for SC17 on. Fit: slumped against the door with his cheek on the headrest, the collar stands clear of the jaw on its stays, so collar and jaw read as two shapes (V01 0-42 s). The shirt is tucked 10 cm deep and flat all round; nothing rides up or gapes. Cuffs buttoned at the wrist bone. Seated, the trouser break rests on the Derby vamp. SC17: seated upright in the armchair, hands on the arms. SC18: seated upright in PR-19.
- **accessories**: PR-04 (set-owned): taken from the SC15 pile and put on him in SC17. It stays on his face to V01-B06, with only the left arm showing to the phone. Lifted off at V01-B07 and never on him again.; Nothing else worn: no watch, ring or chain; hands bare.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: deep, warm undertone, #6E4631 approx, never lightened by makeup, the grade or the 35mm look. The same hex desaturated, with a grey-ash wash at the lips, the lids and under the eyes. Lips dry and matte, nail beds greyed. Pores, forehead lines and crow's feet show. Eyes (V01-B07, B08, V02-B04 to B06, SC18): open, the upper lid at the top edge of the iris in every shot. Irises dark brown #2A1A12, unchanged. The cornea is dry and dulled with a faint grey film and no wet shine (joint lock with VFX). From V01-B07, dust #C9A45C sits on the lashes, in the lid creases, the forehead lines and the crow's feet, matching the collar. Hair: black tight 4A curls, the top 12-15 cm stretched and picked out at the crown, so in the wind (B07, V02-B04) the top shifts as one mass and the crown ends flutter. Sides 1 cm, no part, a light dust film at the hairline in V01. Beard 3 mm with the hard cheek line, unchanged. The mole on the left side of the neck stays uncovered. SC16, face-down: the same grey-ash at the lips, mouth open. Do not lighten him or loosen the curl.
- **continuity locks**: [phone frame 0-42 s, V02, S01] Top button fastened: seen from his left side, the collar band runs unbroken to the front (the reverse of JEAN_L01's open top button in SC04-SC15). Collar stand clear of the jaw.; [phone frame, V01 0-42 s] Mole on the LEFT side of the neck, 2 cm above the collar band, facing camera. A mole on the right means the frame is mirrored: reject it. Only the left sunglasses arm shows, up to B06.; [phone frame] Collar dust follows PR-57: C1 at B01, C2 at B02, C3 from B05.; [close-up] Eyes open, upper lid at the top edge of the iris, no blink, dust on the lashes, no wet shine.; [wide] All black, tucked flat with no blouse over the belt, cuffs buttoned, no jewellery.; [medium, from SC17] One horizontal fold crease across the chest, 12 cm below the collar.

### BARTENDER_L01 (BARTENDER)

- **garment silhouette**: Closet: work clothes bought in multipacks and worn until they give out. He spends on his shoes because he stands for ten hours. Story: Thursday late shift, early October 2026 (assumed), SC04-SC08, the bar at about 24 °C: short sleeves, forearms bare. Head to toe: a slate short-sleeve crew-neck T-shirt, tucked in, sleeves to mid-bicep. A tobacco bib apron with a neck strap: the bib reaches the top of the sternum, the hem sits at the knee, and the ties are knotted at the back. Charcoal work trousers, mid rise, plain hem. Dark grey socks. Brown-black slip-resistant leather work shoes. Older than the story: the apron.
- **materials colors**: T-shirt: heavyweight cotton single jersey 220 g/m², solid, matte, slate #56657A (approx L*42, C*14, h 268°). Apron: waxed cotton duck 340 g/m², tobacco #6A4E36 (approx L*35); the neck strap and ties are sewn on, with no hardware. Older than the story: the wax is worn off in pale cracks #8A7058 along the waist fold and at the corner of the bib pocket, and the ties are hand-darkened for 20 cm either side of the knot. To rebuild, flex and sand the wax. Trousers: cotton twill 280 g/m², charcoal #3D3E40. Socks: cotton, dark grey #4A4B4C. Shoes: leather, brown-black #2E2724, plain rubber sole. Pen: plain black plastic #1F1F21, matte, no print. Bar towel: cotton huckaback, off-white #E6E2D8. No logo, bar name or lettering on any garment, the pen or the towel (A10).
- **fit notes**: 170 cm, US men's XL: barrel chest, shoulders and hips the same width. T-shirt: shoulder seam 2 cm past the shoulder point, 12 cm ease. The sleeve hem sits at mid-bicep, so both forearms are bare to above the elbow for the tab-slip flick and the pours. Tucked. Apron: neck strap at the base of the neck, bib flat to the top of the sternum, ties at the natural waist. From the stools, the 107 cm counter hides everything below the apron waist. Trousers: 2 cm ease, the plain hem touching the shoe.
- **accessories**: Hands and forearms bare: no ring, watch or bracelet.; The black pen clipped in the bib pocket.; The off-white bar towel tucked into the apron tie at his right hip.; Set-owned: the PR-34 tab slip and tray.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: medium, warm undertone, #B07B55 approx. No base. Left uncovered: the deep forehead lines, the crow's feet, the broken capillaries across the nose, and the pale scar splitting the outer end of the left brow. Head shaved to 1 mm, with grey stubble #8E8882 approx at the sides and back. A full grey-black moustache #4A4642 approx with grey through it, trimmed to the corners of the mouth and no further. No beard; jaw shaved. Heavy greying brows, untouched. Do not add hair or de-age him.
- **continuity locks**: [medium] Slate sleeves at mid-bicep, both forearms bare. Tobacco bib apron on, neck strap at the base of the neck.; [close-up] Head shaved to 1 mm with grey stubble at the sides. Moustache to the corners of the mouth. No beard.; [any frame] No lettering or logo on the tee, apron, pen or towel.

### DRIVER_L01 (DRIVER)

- **garment silhouette**: Closet: comfortable, bought once and kept. He spends nothing on clothes and keeps his car cleaner than his shirts. Story: Thursday night just after last call (about 01:47), early October 2026 (assumed), SC09. The cabin is at about 20 °C with the air on: short sleeves. Head to toe: a sage short-sleeve button-front shirt, point collar, 6 buttons, top button open, tucked in. A white crew undershirt shows at the open neck. Grey-brown twill trousers, mid rise, brown belt. Grey socks. Brown leather walking shoes. Everything below the chest is out of the dash-cam frame. Older than the story: the shirt.
- **materials colors**: Shirt: cotton poplin 115 g/m², solid, sage #7D8A74 (approx L*56, C*14, h 131°), with tonal plastic buttons #76826E, matte. Older than the story: the left shoulder and left sleeve are sun-faded to #97A28E from the driver's window, and the collar edges are soft. To rebuild, bleach and sand the left side. It is a detail, not an identity mark, because models mirror. Undershirt: cotton single jersey 150 g/m², white #E9E7E1 (approx L*92). Trousers: cotton twill 260 g/m², grey-brown #6E665A. Belt: leather, brown #4A3A2E; buckle in brushed steel #8C8B87 approx, satin. Socks: cotton, grey #6A6A68. Shoes: leather, brown #4A3A2E, plain rubber sole. No rideshare badge, lanyard, logo or lettering (A10).
- **fit notes**: About 180 cm, US men's M: narrow sloping shoulders, thin arms. Shirt: the shoulder seam lands 1 cm past the shoulder point because of the slope; 14 cm ease. The sleeve hem sits at mid-bicep, so his right forearm and hand are bare when they rise into the dome light. Tucked, with the collar open one button to show the undershirt's crew. He sits upright with both hands on the wheel, and his shoulder line reads as a clear slope against the side window.
- **accessories**: Hands and wrists bare: no ring or watch. His right hand rises to the dome switch.; No glasses, lanyard, badge or rideshare marks (A10).; Set-owned: the PR-36 dash cam and the PR-37 dome light.
- **hair makeup**: Skin: light, warm undertone, #DDBBA3 approx. Left uncovered: the red weathering across the cheeks and nose and the brown age spot at the left temple. No base. The crown scalp is left unpowdered, so it shines under the dome light. Hair: thinning silver #CFC9C0 approx, straight, cropped to 1.5 cm, receding high, no part. Clean-shaven. Heavy grey brows, untouched. His right hand keeps its raised veins, age spots and swollen knuckles.
- **continuity locks**: [dash-cam frame] Sage shirt, top button open, white undershirt crew showing. Right sleeve at mid-bicep, forearm bare.; [dash-cam frame] Silver crop receding high, crown scalp showing. Clean-shaven. No glasses.; [dash-cam frame] No badge, lanyard or lettering anywhere on him.

## Director's treatment

### SC01

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat B07 (42.0-50.0): close on her right hand turning his chin. His open eyes are the one face shot (taste note: hands before faces). She wants him to look back at her. Two things stop her: he is dead, and she won't turn the lens on herself. B01 (0-4), to wake him to the drive: she laughs loud on the mic, the way you laugh at a lover sleeping through the best part, and the frame jolts. B02 (4-10), to make him presentable: a full stop 50 cm short of the untouched drift (PR-58), at a sign nobody else will reach. Her left hand brushes the dust off him and flattens his collar, rests a beat, then pulls away. Real detail: she obeys a stop line on an empty road. B03 (10-18), hold: three jumps and the pose never changes. That is the joke. B04 (18-26), to get into the picture: her right thumb rests 2.2 s on the flip icon, played as shyness, then slides off, and she laughs. B05 (26-34), to keep seeing him as the light goes: the video light comes on. B06 (34-42), her face for the first time: she laughs at the wheel with her eyes on him. Her eyes never go to the road or any lens. No knowing look, no lip bite. B07: she wants him to look at her, and the dark is her excuse to take off his sunglasses. Her laugh stops and her fingers stay on his chin 1.2 s. This is not shock; she already knows. B08 (50-56), back behind the lens: she hooks his sunglasses at her own neckline, laughs softer, and her thumb rests only 1.2 s ('not like this, not yet'). JEAN, the performance and generation rule for every dead beat in the pilot: his head moves only when her hand moves it, stays where she leaves it, and never lolls or bobs with the car or her laugh. No blink, no breath.
- **blocking**: LOC-01 with the top down, in LOC-02, heading north in the northbound lane. Low sun in the west, behind the driver's side. VENUS sits in the driver seat (y 32), left hand at the worn 10 o'clock arc. PR-01 is upright in her right hand at arm's length, her wrist on the PR-57 arc at the dash-top centre. The lens aims east across Jean's left shoulder, so PR-11 and PR-10 sit behind his head. JEAN is in pose P1: cheek on the passenger pad (x 108, y 100), face to the passenger door (east), PR-04 on. The lens sees only collar, left jaw, left sunglasses arm and neck mole. The wind is relative, nose to tail. B02: front tyres 50 cm short of PR-58, with PR-10 about 3 m ahead of and east of his head. Line of action: the road, the car always heading north. Every full-width setup stays west of the car's centreline. B06 and B07 up to 45.0 are shot from the west (driver's) side: she is nearest the lens, and he shows the back of his head and the glasses. From 45.0 the camera is ahead of the windscreen, on or just west of the centreline, looking back south. Her chin turn takes his face from the door (bearing 90°) to bearing about 30°: 60° off the door and 30° short of dead ahead. From this axis it reads as a three-quarter with both eyes, and his gaze runs past the lens on its east side. His head stays there for the rest of V01 and into SC18. B08: the phone is held forward of his face on the windscreen side, a hand's width west of his gaze line, lens aimed back at his eyes, so they sit centred and aim past the lens. 9:16: the phone-frame shots are native upright. In B07 her hand and his face stay inside the middle third on both axes, because V02-B03 uses 42.8-43.8 and 45.0-46.0.
- **action progression**: Entry at 0.0: PR-01 recording, light off; collar C1; dash D2; PR-04 on him; PR-61 in the ignition. B01, 0.0-4.0, one plate: hold on collar and jaw (1.8); a full-torso laugh jolts the frame (1.6); the frame settles (0.6). B02, 4.0-10.0: the car rolls to a full stop and the frame dips (0.8); her left hand leaves the wheel and enters under the lens (0.7); two strokes take the collar from C1 to C2 (1.2); she pinches the left point flat (1.0); her hand rests (0.8); hidden cut at 8.9 as the withdrawing hand wipes the lens (0.4); the frame lurches away over PR-58 (1.1). Plates: 4.0-8.9 and 8.9-10.0. B03, 10.0-18.0, three plates: 10.0-12.6 storefronts (the loop's second pass), 12.6-15.3 open desert, 15.3-18.0 scrub hills. Each cut lands on a snatch of laugh, and the dust builds toward C3. B04, 18.0-26.0: hold (0.8); right thumb onto the icon (1.0); rests (2.2); slides to the frame edge (0.8); laugh jolt at 22.8, with the plate join at 22.9 hidden in its blur; laugh (2.0); settles on him (1.2). B05, 26.0-34.0, REC 01:04:29: noisy dusk picture (2.7); the side-button press happens off picture (0.3); at 29.0 the light comes on and the plate join hides in the exposure pop; C3 dust glints with the car's vibration (5.0). From 26.0 the phone records unbroken to 52.0. B06, 34.0-42.0: the upright frame still holds his turned head at REC 01:04:37 (0.8, 19 frames); she lowers the phone and the pillars open to full width, phone compression giving way to 35mm film (0.6); the phone lies face up in her lap, still recording, light on; her right hand rests on her thigh; she laughs at the wheel, eyes on him (6.6), with the plate join at 39.8 inside the laugh. B07, 42.0-50.0. Plate 1, west side: her right hand goes from her thigh to his face (0.8); lifts PR-04 off by its left arm, clear of his right ear (1.2); drops to his jaw, the glasses open in her palm (1.0). Cut on action at 45.0 to the front axis as two fingers meet his chin. Plate 2, 45.0-50.0: head and shoulders turn together under her fingers, the collar shifting with the jaw, and stop at bearing 30° with no loll (1.4); her fingers stay (1.2); they withdraw out of frame (0.4); hold on the open eyes, hair moving, no blink (2.0). Her left hand stays on the wheel throughout. B08, 50.0-56.0. Plate 1, front axis, 50.0-52.0: her right hand hooks PR-04's open left arm into her neckline, lenses out (1.0); lifts PR-01 from her lap (0.6); the frame closes to upright, film to phone (0.4). Plate 2, phone frame, 52.0-56.0, REC 01:04:55: his eyes centred under the light; laugh shake (1.0); thumb to the icon (0.8); rests (1.2); slides back (0.6); hold (0.4). Generate plate 2 long for V02.
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before (B01-B06): laughing, filming, eyes on him. Trigger (B07), which the camera sees: she turns his chin and his open eyes land at bearing 30°, past the lens and never on her. After: no laugh for 8 s, and her fingers stay on his chin 1.2 s. In B08 she takes his sunglasses to herself and goes back behind the lens. The laugh returns softer and the thumb rest is shorter (1.2 s against 2.2 s), so the same gesture now reads 'not like this, not yet'. B04's rest is a hold: no trigger, same state, shyness. Up to B07 the viewer's shift from lover to dead man is carried by his stillness, not by any change in her.
- **transition in**: The pilot opens with no fade: from black straight into the phone's upright frame at REC 00:00:04. Her laugh and the wind on the mic start on frame one.
- **transition out**: Hard cut with a time jump to last light. B08's phone frame (thumb leaving the icon, wind on the mic) cuts to B09's locked-off carport, full width on 35mm. The mic wind cuts dead and the cooling engine's ping starts on the cut. The eye goes from his eyes at the centre of the upright frame to the car under the MOTEL sign, also at centre.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B04 and B08 carry it: twice her right thumb rests on the flip-camera icon over him, then slides back. Cut this and the viewer loses the want, a woman who films a man for an hour and can't turn the lens round. Without it, the wall, the table for two and SC18's press read as a collector's trophies.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Phone UI (REC dot, timer, flip icon), B01-B06 and B08. Method: composited in finishing over the G plates; original design, clearance pending (A10). Acceptance: the timer reads 00:00:04, 00:06:12, 00:21:40, 00:38:15, 00:52:30, 00:53:10, 01:04:29 running to 01:04:37 at 34.0 (19 frames only), and 01:04:55 from 52.0. It never jumps inside the unbroken 26.0-52.0 take. No OS or maker marks. Risk: generated digits or icons come out garbled.; Thumb on the icon (B04, B08; V02-B06). Method: a practical plate of a hand double's right thumb (the 6 mm nick) over a blank phone on green, composited over G. Acceptance: the thumb enters nail-side and rests 2.2 s (B04) or 1.2 s (B08) without a twitch. It never presses, so the UI never changes state before SC18. Risk: a generated thumb presses or morphs and spends SC18's payoff. Fallback: the script's under-a-second front-camera flip, her eyes on him and never in the lens.; Head rule (Jean, all of V01). Method: G, with one locked head plate reused where the pose holds. Acceptance: through B01-B06 his head holds P1 within a few pixels in every plate, with no bob from the car or from her laugh jolts. In B07 it moves only during the 1.4 s her fingers are on it. From 46.4 it holds at bearing 30° through B08. Risk: a bob reads alive; a loll reads as a rag doll.; Collar touch, B02 (V02-B01). Method: G; the hand is risky. Acceptance: one hand, two strokes; the collar points go from dusted to #111111 while the shoulders keep their film (C1 to C2); his head does not move under her touch. Fallback: her hand rests on his collar with no adjusting.; Dead eyes, B07-B08 (V02-B04/B05). Method: G, locked in composite. Acceptance: zero blinks and zero eye movement across 45.0-50.0 and 52.0-56.0; upper lids at the top edge of the iris; dry, dulled corneas with dust on the lashes; gaze at bearing 30°, past the lens on its east side; hair moving while the eyes hold. If a take fails, freeze the eye region from the best frame and keep the hair and background live. Risk: one blink kills the reveal. A16: first try in the moving car. Fallback: parked at the motel, where the south wind lifts his hair toward the windscreen; don't match it to the moving plates.; Axis change at 45.0, from the west side to ahead of the windscreen. Acceptance: both setups sit west of the car's centreline; Venus is nearer the lens in both; the front-axis plate reads as a three-quarter with both eyes, not a profile. Risk: a setup east of the centreline crosses the line and puts his gaze into the lens.; Three-step hand chains, B07 (lift the glasses, hold them open, turn the chin) and B08 (hook the glasses, lift the phone, thumb). Method: G; hands risky. Acceptance: PR-04 stays in her right hand across the 45.0 cut and is hooked, lenses out, by 51.0; it never vanishes or doubles. Fallback: already hooked at 50.0, cut on the lift.; Vehicle. Method: G. Acceptance: one open 1950s-shape two-seater per LOC-01, left-hand drive, blank rear plate, no badges, no numerals on the dials, northbound in the right lane; no other vehicle, person or animal in any frame. Risk: a real make's grille or badge (A10).; Jump-cut registration, B03. Method: one locked G of Jean over varied background plates. Acceptance: head, collar and sunglasses overlay within a few pixels; only the sun's height and the background change.; PR-10 'STOP': composite the letters if the generator distorts them. No other legible text in LOC-02.; Laugh sync, B06, the only sync in V01. Acceptance: the laugh peaks align with the visible torso shakes within 2 frames. One laugh voice is locked across V01, V02 and D01.; Hair in wind: hers is held (stylist) and never crosses the hand shots. His top hair must move as one mass in B07: it is the life in the frame that the eyes don't have.; No slow motion anywhere; everything plays at 24 fps real speed (taste note).; Frame open and close (B06, B08): a graphic transition built in finishing. The pillars open in 0.6 s and close in 0.4 s, riding the phone's own motion, with the texture switching at the same moment (phone compression inside, 35mm grain outside). No dissolve.; S01 comes from this scene's gold light, before B04, staged from behind her right shoulder: the phone shows Jean under the REC dot, the real Jean beside it in P1, and she laughs in three-quarter profile with her eyes on him, never the lens. This breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose, as the script decided: a poster needs its lead.

### SC02

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat B10: close on the unbroken dust as she leans over the office step to tug the padlock rather than set a foot on it. She wants proof that nobody has found the motel, and she can't bring him in until she has it. B09 (56-60), to arm herself: she leans across his knees to the glovebox and takes the pistol by the grip, muzzle down and away from him throughout, then seats it grip-up in her right back pocket. Real detail: trained handling, even beside a dead man. B10 (60-64), to check: she walks the frontage alone, glances once at the empty highway, tugs the padlock twice, then looks down at the dust. B11 (64-72), to move in: one fast trip with both arms full, no look back at him, and she shoulders the swollen door. JEAN: still, his face set where B07 left it; her lean doesn't move him.
- **blocking**: LOC-03 at last light. The wind comes from the highway (south), and the fronds lean toward the room block. The car is nosed in facing north at x 21.0-22.55, y 13.5-17.5, its passenger side (Jean) toward room 3's door PR-15 (x 25.7-26.6) and the boot at the south end. Entry: VENUS in the driver seat, engine off. PR-01 is in her right front jeans pocket and PR-61 in her left (both set across the cut); PR-04 hangs at her neckline; PR-02 is in the glovebox, muzzle toward the passenger door. B09: she leans east across Jean's knees to the glovebox (y 76-106). The lid drops and stays open. B10: getting out of the driver door is elided. She walks west along the frontage to the office door (x 12, south face) and stops on the gravel at the east end of PR-14, toes off the step. The way back to the car runs east and is not seen. B11: from the boot north-east along her worn track (40 cm, packed dust) to PR-15. She shoulders the latch edge at the rubbed patch (143-173 cm); the door sticks at 35°, then opens to 90°. The bags drop inside under the boarded window (x 40-140, y 20-48).
- **action progression**: B09, 56.0-60.0, one plate: hold on the engine ping (0.4); she leans across his knees, right hand to the glovebox (0.9); thumbs the latch and the lid drops (0.4); lifts PR-02 out by the grip, muzzle down (0.7); sits back upright (0.8); lifts her right hip and slides it grip-up into her right back pocket, 6 cm showing (0.8). Both hands end free. Hard cut. B10, 60.0-64.0, one plate: the last 2.3 m of her walk west, grip showing, one glance south at the highway (1.8); she stops at the step's edge (0.5); leans and tugs PR-13 twice with her right hand, and it knocks on the hasp and stays locked (0.7); she straightens and looks down at the unbroken dust (1.0). Hard cut. B11, 64.0-72.0. Plate A, 64.0-68.0, at the boot: lid up (0.8); PR-05 onto her left shoulder (1.1); PR-06 onto her right shoulder (1.0); both PR-07 sacks hugged to her chest (1.1). Jean stays in shot and the boot is left open. Hard cut. Plate B, 68.0-72.0, at PR-15: she enters laden on her last step (0.4); turns her right shoulder to the rubbed patch (0.4); first shove, the door sticks at 35° (0.7); second shove, it scrapes to 90° (1.0); she steps in (0.7); shrugs both bags off under the window (0.8). The sacks stay against her chest.
- **emotional shift**: Before: quick and armed, with one glance at the highway during the walk. Trigger, which the camera sees: the unbroken dust on PR-14. After: she turns back without another look at the road and unloads at speed, both shoulders loaded. She checks nothing else for the rest of the cold open.
- **transition in**: Hard cut with a time jump from SC01's phone frame to the full-width carport on 35mm. The cooling-engine ping replaces the mic wind.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the door scraping open. Her back goes into the dark doorway at frame centre, and we cut to inside the black room, where the lantern click lights the wall (SC03). The scrape carries over the cut, and the wind in the palms becomes wind at the door.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B11 carries it: she carries his bag beside hers into the room, moving in with him rather than stashing a body. Cut this and the viewer loses that the motel is a home she has kept and checked for the two of them. The pistol and the untouched dust are her guarding it; without them the room reads as a lair for trophies instead of a house for two.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Pistol PR-02, B09-B10. Method: G, or a practical plate if the generator can't hold its shape. Acceptance: the muzzle never crosses Jean or the lens; grip up with 6 cm showing in the right back pocket, readable against light-wash denim (#8FA3B8, stylist); no markings. Risk: a morphing or camera-pointed gun reads as femme-fatale gloss. Fallback: already in the pocket, no hand on it.; Leaning over the dead body, B09, a five-step chain. Acceptance: Jean's knees and head do not move while she leans across him (head rule). Risk: he shifts and reads alive.; PR-14 dust. Acceptance: no footprint on the step in any frame of B10 or later.; Carry, B11. Method: G. Acceptance: exactly two shoulder bags and two sacks (the full jugs ride inside sack 2, per PR-07); nothing appears or vanishes across the 68.0 cut.; PR-12: 'MOTEL' and 'OFFICE' verbatim, composited if garbled; no other text. The loose panel may move and tick.; Door, B11. Method: G. Acceptance: two shoves and one stop at 35°, not a single swing.

### SC03

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands and the chair legs as she drags the second chair up and squares it beside hers. She wants the table set for two. Play her hands; her head stays above the lantern's pool and never turns to the door (taste note: hands before faces). She unpacks fast, in pairs and handfuls, like someone who has done it many times. She gives one look, across the table at the bare boards where his chair goes, and she slows only for the chair. Then she leaves to bring him in. The held frame has nobody in it.
- **blocking**: LOC-04. The planned B13/S02 view comes from the door end (wall A) facing wall C; exact placement is the DP's. PR-15 stands open at 90°. PR-16 is already on the table, off, at x 180, y 365. She works standing at the table's south edge around x 190, y 305, facing north, so everything is within reach and she reads in silhouette against the lit wall, with wall C clear above and either side of her. Placements: PR-25 at x 145, y 340, slot south; PR-24 at x 195-225, y 340-360; PR-21 in two stacks at x 182-198, y 382-397; PR-22 at x 206-238, y 382-397. The empty sacks go crushed under the table. Her one look is straight across the table at the bare boards at x 210, y 420 beside PR-18 (x 150, y 420). PR-19 travels from wall D (x 315-360, y 280-325, facing 270°) round the table's north-east corner to x 210, y 420, facing south. Exit: south past the table's east end and out through the lens toward the door, a hidden cut. The bare pine on wall C at x 280-345 stays empty.
- **action progression**: Entry at 72.0: dark room, sacks against her chest, bags already under the window. B12, 72.0-82.0. Plate A, 72.0-76.0: sacks onto the table's south edge (0.6); her right hand clicks the lantern slider and the wall of photos appears (0.5); cans out in pairs into two stacks of three (1.5); oranges out of sack 2 in a loose cluster (0.7); both full jugs out of sack 2, one per hand, side by side (0.7). Hard cut. Plate B, 76.0-78.5: the printer out of sack 1 and set square, slot to the door (0.8); her head lifts to the bare boards across the table and holds (0.9); sacks crushed and pushed under the table with a foot (0.8). Cut into the action at 78.5 as her body turns east; the walk to wall D is elided. Plate C, 78.5-82.0: PR-19 dragged by its back over the last 0.8 m round the north-east corner (1.0); both hands square it beside PR-18 (0.7); one nudge levels the seat edges (0.3); both hands rest on its back (0.7); she walks south past the lens, her body wiping frame, for the hidden cut at 82.0 (0.8). B13, 82.0-88.0: a locked hold where nothing moves; the wind works the open door behind camera. B14, 88.0-90.0: the same frame. The LIMERENCE card lands and the wind cuts to silence at 88.0.
- **emotional shift**: Before: fast unpacking, her hands never pause. Trigger, which the camera sees: as she sets the printer, her head lifts to the bare boards across the table, the one empty place. After: her hands slow. The chair gets 2.7 s against 1.5 s for six cans, with two hands to square it, one nudge, and both hands resting 0.7 s on its back, the only rest in the scene, before she goes for him. B13-B14 are a hold: nobody is in frame and the room carries it.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from SC02's doorway into the dark room; the door scrape carries across. The lantern click reveals the room.
- **transition out**: V01: the LIMERENCE card sits on the held frame in silence, then cut to black at 90.0. In the pilot: a hard cut from the card's silence to SC04's upright phone frame. Nothing matches across the cut; instead SC04's first frame echoes V01-B01's collar-and-jaw framing, with Jean alive.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-20 over the table carries it, with PR-19 pulled up beside PR-18: a wall of him alone, none of them together, above a table set for two. Cut this and the viewer loses the gap the idea lives in, hundreds of photos and not one of her with him, and the printer (PR-25) that SC18 pays off.
- **practical or compositing flags**: PR-20 wall (verified fact: none of them together). Method: composite. Generate or shoot about 40 unique long-lens images of the locked JEAN (bar window, unbranded gas pump, crossing a street; half night, half day; always in black), tiled to 250 landscape prints on the world bible's grid as a CG texture on the pine. Acceptance at S02 resolution: no print contains a second person or any woman; no legible text; Jean in black in every print; the pine at x 280-345 left bare. Risk: a freely generated wall invents couple photos and breaks the fact.; Her position at the south edge. Acceptance: in the B13-master view her silhouette never hides more than the lower centre of wall C, and the printer and the chair reads stay clear. The DP confirms placement. Risk: the reveal of the wall is blocked.; Held frame, B13-B14 and S02. Method: lock one frame and hold it as a still; the lantern runs on batteries, so nothing flickers. Acceptance: zero pixel motion from 82.0 to 90.0 on a flat difference matte. Risk: generator drift, floating dust or breathing light.; Hands unpacking. Method: G across plates of 4.0 and 2.5 s. Acceptance: the counts hold (6 cans, 2 full jugs, 5 oranges, 1 printer, 9 empty jugs by the door), and nothing appears on the table without a hand placing it. Risk: a hand merges with a can or a jug.; Chair. Acceptance: PR-19 ends square beside PR-18, seat edges level, backs below the photos' 95 cm bottom edge; the drag reads slower than the unpacking.; Title: LIMERENCE only, final 2 s, silent, composited. S02 is the B13 frame with no text.

### V02

- **performance beats**: No new performance; V02 is rebuilt from SC01's plates. It stays Venus's piece, seen through her phone. Hero beat V02-B06: her thumb rests on the flip icon over his open eyes, then slides back, and it has to read with the sound off.
- **blocking**: Native 9:16. The phone-frame plates (V02-B01, B02, B04-B06) are the upright frame at full height, so nothing is restaged, but they must be generated at 1080x1920 or better so V02 isn't an upscale. V02-B03 is cut from V01-B07's two axes (42.8-43.8 west side, 45.0-46.0 front axis), with her right hand and his face held in the middle third of both. V02-B04 pushes in on the B08 phone plate until his eyes fill the frame.
- **action progression**: B01, 0.0-1.5: V01 5.5-7.0, the brush and the collar, REC 00:06:13. B02, 1.5-4.0: V01 15.3-17.8, scrub hills, REC 00:52:30. B03, 4.0-6.0: V01 42.8-43.8 (the glasses lift, west side), then a jump to 45.0-46.0 (the chin turn, front axis); 35mm texture, no UI. B04, 6.0-9.0: a handle from the B08 eyes plate, pushed in and still, teaser clock from REC 01:04:55. B05, 9.0-11.0: the laugh shake, from REC 01:04:58. B06, 11.0-13.0: from REC 01:05:00 the thumb rests (1.2) and slides back (0.6); the mic cuts out (0.2). B07, 13.0-15.0: the frame goes black, the REC dot is gone, and LIMERENCE sits centred.
- **emotional shift**: The same shift as SC01, compressed. Before: care (the collar) and time (the timer jump). Trigger: his open eyes under the light (B04). After: her laugh shakes the frame while his eyes stay still (B05), and her thumb rests and retreats (B06). With the sound off, the timer and the unblinking eyes carry time and death, and the thumb carries the want.
- **transition in**: It opens straight on the phone frame mid-action at the collar, with wind on the mic from frame one.
- **transition out**: The recording stops: hard cut to black at 13.0 with the mic wind cut dead, then LIMERENCE in silence to 15.0.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' B06 carries it: her thumb on the flip icon over his open eyes, sliding back. Cut it and V02 becomes a dead-body reveal; the want, and the reason to open V01, are gone.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Footage length. V01-B08's phone section runs 4.0 s, but V02-B04 to B06 need about 7 s on his eyes. Method: generate the eyes plate with handles (two plates of up to 5 s joined on a laugh jolt), and shoot the thumb plate long. Acceptance: no frame used twice in V02.; Teaser clock. Acceptance: V02's timer runs continuously within each beat and follows the script's teaser values, deliberately not frame-matched to V01.; Muted read. Acceptance: with the sound off, a test viewer reads 'a man who never wakes, and she won't turn the camera on herself'. The REC timer is the only on-screen text besides the title.; Push-in resolution: generate the eyes plate at 4K equivalent so the B04 push keeps the dull corneas and the dust on the lashes.

### SC04

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands as the pistol goes into the glovebox and the tin comes out. She wants to go in before he leaves alone again, and walking into his frame means going without the gun. Tactic 1: from V01-B01's collar-and-jaw framing she zooms toward his face until the lens runs out and the picture breaks into grain, and she keeps it there. Real detail: she'd rather have his face soft than the room sharp. Tactic 2: she waits while he raises two fingers for another. Trigger: the BARTENDER sets the tab slip face down in front of him, and she moves at once. Jean's living face stays soft and grainy inside her phone, a picture rather than a performance shot.
- **blocking**: LOC-01 with the top up, passenger curtain fitted, driver's curtain off, parked on the far kerb with the driver side to the bar at 14 m. VENUS is in the driver seat: PR-01 upright in her right hand at the open side, PR-02 grip-up in her right back pocket, PR-61 in the ignition. PR-08 (band on) lies on the passenger cushion with PR-09 half under it; PR-03 is closed in the glovebox. Through the window (LOC-05 wall A, x 40-440): JEAN on stool 2 (y 350), PR-33 at x 148, y 395, PR-05 under the stool, the BARTENDER in the aisle. After the trade she gets out of the driver door (the street side, facing the bar) and crosses to the glazed door (x 470-560).
- **action progression**: It opens inside her upright phone frame on his black collar and jaw through the glass, V01-B01's framing, with him alive and lifting his glass (2.0). She zooms until his face breaks into grain (4.0) and holds (to about 0:40). A 1.5 s insert of PR-09. He raises two fingers and the bartender pours (to about 1:00). The bartender sets PR-34 face down at x 150, y 360 (1.0). The trade: phone face down onto PR-08, and the frame opens to full width, phone video to 35mm (0.8); her right hand draws PR-02, hip lifted (1.2); she leans right and her left hand drops the glovebox lid (0.8); her right hand lays PR-02 in, muzzle toward the passenger door (0.8); takes PR-03 (0.6); her left hand shuts the lid (0.4); her right hand pockets the tin in her left jacket pocket (1.0); PR-01 into her right chest pocket (0.8); her left hand pulls PR-61 from the ignition into her left front jeans pocket (0.8). Out of the door (2.0) and across the street (4.0). Her hands end empty.
- **emotional shift**: Before: still, phone up, zoomed in, barely blinking. Trigger: the tab slip lands in her frame. After: she lowers the phone for the first time and her hands move fast, the only hurry in the pilot until SC17's scrub.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from V01's silent held card. Nothing matches; SC04's first frame repeats V01-B01's framing (upright phone frame, REC dot, black collar and jaw) with him alive. Muffled bar noise through the glass and street sound come in on the cut.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: her hand hits the bar door's glass from outside, and SC05 opens inside as the door swings in, Jean's back in the foreground. Street sound becomes bar room tone across the cut.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-08 and the lowered phone carry it: months of him alone, all shot from behind her lens, and then she puts the phone down and walks into his frame. Cut this and the viewer loses where the wall came from, and the first time she steps out from behind the camera toward him.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Her phone screen. Method: a practical plate of Jean through real bar glass, with the UI and the grainy zoom composited. Acceptance: the REC dot and flip icon match V01's UI exactly; Jean stays readable through the reflections at the end of the zoom (soft is fine, lost is not).; PR-09. Acceptance: only 'NO FURTHER ACTION' is legible, in one 1.5 s insert; no agency, seal or case number; fictional (A02).; Pistol and tin swap. Method: practical. Acceptance: the muzzle never points toward her or the lens; the tin is never opened.; Street. Acceptance: no brand, plate or sign is legible (A10).

### SC05

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on the straw as her fingertip lays the first one in the wet ring his glass left. She wants him to agree to her game; JEAN wants to finish his drink without being seen. 'Have we met before?' is a test she already knows the answer to. Play it straight: she is listening for a lie. He tells the truth, and she gives nothing away. Her phone stays in her right chest pocket all scene and her hand never goes to it: the first time she sits in his frame with no lens of hers between them. The Naked and Famous joke lands with her right hand on his forearm and a real laugh, and we cut on the laugh. JEAN starts rounded over his glass with his elbow on the worn patch. The mimosa jab gets to him: he props himself up and turns his stool to her. BARTENDER: eyes on the tab, 'What can I get you guys?' said half to the counter. Real detail: Jean slides the tab slip back unsigned with one finger, without looking at it.
- **blocking**: LOC-05. VENUS comes in by the glazed door (wall A, x 470-560) and walks north behind the stools, behind Jean's back so he doesn't see her coming, to stool 3 (y 440). She turns the stool fully to him and keeps her elbows off the counter. JEAN is on stool 2 (y 350) with PR-05 underneath; the caddy PR-29 is at x 130, y 480 by her elbow. Jean lifts PR-33 off x 148, y 395 and sets it at x 140, y 320, away from her, leaving the wet ring between them. She lays the base straw through the ring, parallel to the counter edge at x 148, y 385-405. PR-32 lands at x 140, y 350 (his) and x 140, y 440 (hers). PR-34 is slid back to x 105, y 360 and taken.
- **action progression**: Her walk from the door to the stool (5.0); she sits and turns the stool (1.5). Dialogue to 'Sure.' Jean drinks and moves his glass away (2.0). Her right hand draws a straw from the caddy (0.8) and lays it flat in the ring (1.0). The rules: her fingertip traces the house outline on the bare counter. Jean's counter, the bartender, her order. Her right hand passes her pocket and lands on his forearm on 'naked' (0.5), stays through 'Kidding' and lifts on the laugh (1.0). He props up and turns his stool (2.0), then slides PR-34 back unsigned (1.0). The bartender sets down the coupes (3.0). The coupes touch on 'Cheers' (1.0).
- **emotional shift**: This shift is Jean's. Before: rounded shoulders, eyes on his glass. Trigger: her hand on his forearm and the mimosa jab. After: he sits up, turns his stool to her and pushes the tab back unsigned. Venus holds the same warm attention throughout; nothing has been tested yet, so nothing moves her.
- **transition in**: Cut on action from SC04: the door swinging in.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the clink of the coupes; the clink rings over into SC06's first question.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-01 carries it: its bare black top edge shows at her chest pocket the whole scene, and her right hand goes to the straws and to his forearm, never to it. Cut it and the viewer loses her first minutes in his picture without her camera, the step that SC18's front camera completes.
- **practical or compositing flags**: PR-01 in her pocket. Method: practical. Acceptance: its top edge reads as bare black glass with no case, as in V01, in at least one two-shot, and her hand never touches it. The stylist confirms the pocket shows it without it falling out.; Wet ring and straw. Method: practical. Acceptance: the base straw lies inside a visible 8 cm wet ring, white on #3A2418.; 'Naked and Famous': clearance of the name is pending with the producer (A10); no bottle label is legible.; Background patrons. Acceptance: low-contrast, and they never cross the straw house or the candle.; Contact (her hand on his forearm): practical only. Nothing is handed from person to person; drinks are set down and the slip is slid.

### SC06

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip, face unchanged, as she drinks and draws the wall straw back out. She wants to hear the truth about his ex; JEAN wants to be the wronged man. 'I was wondering when you'd ask' is a private joke, since she has known his name for months, and she lets it pass as flirting. She lays the wall straw on 'a stick toward our home'. 'Who slayed you, my friend?' is the trial question, played light, leaning in, laughing. The lie comes out easy and with no tell. On 'Packed while I was at work', her eyes drop once to the packed duffel under his stool and come back to him: the verdict, with no voiceover. Real detail: he denies something nobody asked. 'That's a drink.' lands at the same pitch as her jokes. Then she moves closer, not further away: 'Are you willing to save me?', and pulls back laughing.
- **blocking**: Same marks as SC05. PR-05 sits under stool 2 at x 175-225, y 336-364, its zip 20 cm short of closing. Straw house S1 to S2: she lays the wall on his side, x 128-148 at y 385, making an L. S3: she draws it back out and drops it in the caddy (x 130, y 480). Her coupe goes to two-thirds. On 'save me' she slides her stool toward his until the gap between the seats is gone, leans to his right ear, then sits back.
- **action progression**: Names (dialogue). She lays the wall straw (1.0). The 'defeated' exchange and her laugh; cut on the laugh. The lie; her eyes to the duffel and back (1.2). The verdict: she lifts her coupe, takes one swallow and sets it down (2.0); her fingertip draws the wall straw out along the counter (1.0) and drops it in the caddy (0.5). 'That's a no?' 'That's a drink.' Stool slide (1.0); lean to his ear (1.0); the line; she pulls back laughing (1.0); 'My turn.'
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before: laughing, leaning in. Trigger: 'I never once raised my voice', a denial nobody asked for, said over his packed bag. After: she drinks without taking her eyes off him and takes the straw with no change of face, then leans closer. His: he hears it as flirting and leans into her lean.
- **transition in**: The clink from SC05 bridges the cut.
- **transition out**: Cut on action, continuous (SC07 is CONTINUOUS): on 'My turn' his right hand closes on PR-31's glass at x 115, y 260, and SC07 picks the slide up in the same instant. The eye goes from her face pulling back to the flame. Room tone carries.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' The wall straw coming back out carries it: the house for two loses its wall the moment he lies. Cut this and the viewer loses the rule behind her want, that she will only be in the picture with a man who tells the truth, and the night plays as seduction instead of a trial she hopes he passes.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dialogue is practical, live-action sync; no generated lip-sync anywhere.; Straw removal. Method: practical. Acceptance: the base straw stays in its wet ring when the wall comes out.; Duffel glance. Acceptance: the zip gap reads in the insert or over-shoulder; no label or logo.; The lie line is new, not Lucas's (writer's flag). Play it plain.

### SC07

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip as it rests on top of the chimney straw, then slides away, and the straw stays standing. It is the same rest-and-slide as her thumb on the flip icon. She wants a reason to let him pass; JEAN wants her home tonight, and he takes her turn. She holds his eyes and blinks less than he does. On 'tame a beast' / 'What dog doesn't want a true owner?' he awards himself a straw without asking. Then 'Who would have known a stranger could be my next lover?', the 3-second vision, and one shake of her head brings her back to the bar.
- **blocking**: Continuous from SC06: coupes as they were, house at S3 (the base only). JEAN slides PR-31 from x 115, y 260 to x 112, y 418, 13 cm off the roof line on her side. He reaches across her to the caddy (x 130, y 480), his arm crossing in front of her, and draws a straw. He pushes it 1 cm into the wax pool at the rim on his side of the wick, where it stands 19 cm above the wax, leaning 5° toward him and away from the flame (PR-30 S1). Her right fingertip goes on top of it, then off.
- **action progression**: The candle slides (1.5); the flame leans and settles. Questions (dialogue). He reaches across her (1.0), draws a straw (0.5), stands it in the wax (1.0) and lets go; it stands (0.5). Her fingertip rests on its top (1.0) and slides off (0.5); it stays. Her line. An insert, 3.0 s at real speed: her hand on a black shirt against white sheets. Back at the bar, one shake of her head (0.8).
- **emotional shift**: Before: the judge, eyes steady, fingertip over the straws and ready to pull. Trigger: she watches him stand his own straw upright. After: her fingertip rests on it and leaves it standing, the vision comes, and she shakes it off. She has gone from judge to lover.
- **transition in**: Cut on action from SC06: his hand on PR-31's glass, the slide continuing in the same instant.
- **transition out**: Hard cut from her head shake to the street outside the window, silent. The bar sound drops to a muffled street.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her fingertip resting on PR-30 and sliding off carries it: the flip-icon gesture, made while he is alive, on the one straw he stood up for them. Cut this and the viewer loses that her hesitation is want, not caution; her thumb in the car would read as a killer's discipline.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Straw beside a live flame, about 3 cm away for the whole take. Method: practical, set at the pool's rim on Jean's side of the wick and leaning away from the flame, with a fire-safety check on set. Acceptance: no scorch, smoke or curl at the end of the take, so SC12's chimney matches. Risk: it chars and reads as a burn effect.; Straw in soft wax. Method: practical, with a hidden stiffener if production design needs one. Acceptance: it stands 19 cm, leans 5° and survives the fingertip rest. Risk: it topples and reads as pulled.; Vision insert: 3.0 s at 24 fps real speed, no slow motion, no nudity; her hand (right-hand scar), a #111111 shirt, white sheets. Practical, with an intimacy coordinator.; Candle flame: practical pillar only.

### SC08

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on his phone in her hand as she takes it from Jean while he frames the two of them, and photographs him alone. Through the window, play a real flirt and cut on the laughs: he adds straws and she pulls them. At last call Jean wants a picture of the night. She takes the phone out of his hand without a word, pleasantly and quickly, as if doing him a favour. Real detail: he checks the photo of himself, laughs and pockets it, too vain to notice she's missing. His last reach is for the chimney, to end the game as the winner, and she plucks it first.
- **blocking**: Window montage, shot from the street side of wall A's window (x 40-440), with the house at x 130, y 395 through the glass. His fresh coupe is set down on the base straw over the dried ring; it rocks, and he lifts it off, laughing, and sets it back at x 140, y 350, re-wetting the ring (PR-32 S3). The house grows to S4 (six straws), then S5, a roof straw drawn toward the counter edge (the S03 frame), then S6 with the last flat straw drawn out. Inside at last call the counter is bare except PR-31 with PR-30 standing at x 112, y 418. JEAN holds PR-35 out at arm's length in his right hand on its front camera. VENUS takes it in her right hand, leans back on stool 3 out of his frame, shoots him beside PR-30 and hands it back. The BARTENDER reaches for PR-32 and PR-33. She plucks PR-30 into her left jacket pocket beside PR-03. Jean shoulders PR-05. Exit: arms tangled, south through the glazed door to the unbranded rideshare at the bar's kerb (x 0-500, right side to the kerb). Her two-seater stays across the street with the top up (SC17's insert returns it).
- **action progression**: Window: five or six silent shots of 1.5-3.0 s, every cut on a laugh; the coupe rock (1.5); the roof-straw pull (1.5). Last call: he holds his phone out (1.0); she takes it (0.8), leans back out of frame (0.6), frames him and shoots (1.3), hands it back (0.8); he looks, laughs and pockets it (2.0). The bartender reaches (1.0) as Jean goes for the chimney (0.8). She plucks it first (0.5) and pockets it (0.8). He shoulders the duffel (1.0); they stand and tangle arms (2.0), walk out (5.0) and get into the car (4.0).
- **emotional shift**: Hers. Before: laughing, building and pulling straws. Trigger: his front camera frames the two of them, the picture she wants, offered on his terms by a man who lied. After: she takes the phone, takes herself out of the frame and keeps the last straw. His: he wanted a picture of both of them, gets one of himself, and is pleased.
- **transition in**: Hard cut to the window exterior, silent.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: the rideshare's rear door shuts, seen from outside, and SC09 opens inside on the dash cam as the thunk lands.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-35 carries it: he offers her a photograph of the two of them and she takes it out of his hand to shoot him alone. Cut this and the viewer loses that she could have the picture tonight and refuses it; it has to be the right one. That refusal is what makes SC18's press an ending, not a habit.
- **practical or compositing flags**: S03 is this scene's roof-straw pull, and a generated still is high-risk because straws bend, multiply and cross. Method: a practical tabletop shoot, or CG straws composited over a bar plate. Acceptance: exactly 5 straws in line plus 1 under her fingertip, all straight and uncrossed; the base in the re-wet ring; a normal fingertip; no text.; Phone handed between people, twice. Method: practical only, never generated. Acceptance: PR-35's split case and cracked screen tell it apart from PR-01; the screen is composited if seen.; Window glass. Method: practical. Acceptance: the house reads through the glass, white straws on the dark counter.; Rideshare: no badges, stickers or trade dress (A10). No slow motion on the laughs.

### SC09

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene, seen from the dash cam. Hero beat: she takes his wrist off the pocket with the tin and moves his hand to her waist, laughing against his mouth. She wants the tin to stay hers to open; JEAN wants his hands inside her jacket. The DRIVER wants his back seat kept clean: he flicks the dome light on without turning his head, and when she flicks it off he leaves it off, his eyes going to the mirror for 2 s at a time. When her eyes open they stay on Jean and never go to the dash-cam lens; she looks into no lens until SC18.
- **blocking**: LOC-06. JEAN gets in first and slides across to y 100, behind the driver. VENUS sits by the kerb door at y 40, her left jacket pocket (tin and chimney) on his side, PR-01 in her right chest pocket. PR-05 lies on its side in the footwell at x 72-100, y 45-95. PR-36 at the top of the windscreen, lens facing rearward; the DRIVER front left. PR-37 over the bench at x 55, y 75, within reach of her right hand; the master rocker is on the driver's front console.
- **action progression**: They kiss with their clothes on, the timestamp running from 01:47:12. The driver's right hand goes to the console rocker and the dome comes on (0.8). Her right hand goes up to the lens rocker and switches it off (1.0) without breaking the kiss. The driver looks to the mirror (2.0), then back to the road. Jean's right hand goes into her jacket and down to the left pocket (1.0); his fingers find the tin's edge through the fabric (0.5). Her left hand closes on his wrist (0.4) and moves it to her waist (0.8); she laughs against his mouth (1.0). Her eyes stay open for the rest of the ride.
- **emotional shift**: Before: eyes shut, all in. Trigger: his hand on the tin's hard edge, visible through the jacket. After: her eyes open, she steers his hand away and laughs, and her eyes stay open until the car stops.
- **transition in**: The door thunk bridges the cut into the dash-cam frame.
- **transition out**: Hard cut from the dark cabin to the white marble lobby (SC10). The jump in brightness carries the move into her world; the first sound is the brass street door closing.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-36 carries it: the only recording of the two of them together tonight belongs to a stranger, and she keeps her eyes off it. Cut this and the viewer loses that not every picture of them counts; hers has to be taken by her, on her terms. They also lose the moment the tin nearly slips out of her control.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dash-cam overlay: composite only the running time from 01:47:12; no date, speed, GPS or brand.; Clothed kissing: practical, with an intimacy coordinator; nothing from it goes into the pitch pieces.; Dome light. Method: practical, with two rockers. Acceptance: on for under 3 s.; Car: no badges or rideshare trade dress (A10).

### SC10

- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on the shoes as he stops in black on the white threshold, toes off his shoes, and her hands line them up on the white mat beside his duffel. He wants to know who she is and how she pays for this, but he wants the night more. His eyes go to the surfaces first: marble, brass. 'Mind the step' cuts off 'So what do you-'; he looks down at the curtail step, and the question is gone. VENUS leads a step ahead, never looks back, and plays 'It's a little messy' as a genuine apology in a spotless flat. Once his shoes are squared she stands, steps back to the distance she holds her phone at and looks at them, hands empty.
- **blocking**: LOC-07: in through the street double door (wall A, x 300-480), along the gloss path to the curtail step (x 660). Flight 1 runs north along wall D to the half landing; flight 2 runs south to the floor-2 landing. PR-39 is on the landing's east side, facing west, and opens inward. She stays a step ahead. LOC-08, from the door: JEAN on the threshold; PR-40 at x 65-145, y 5-55; the bathroom door on wall B at y 120-200, on his left. PR-05 goes flat at x 150-200, y 5-33, outside pocket up, under PR-62. After squaring PR-41 she steps back about 60 cm east of the mat, into the room, and looks.
- **action progression**: Lobby crossing (6.0), his eyes on the floor and the mailboxes. The line is cut off at the step (1.0). Stairs (20.0), dialogue on flight 2. The door opens (1.5). He stands on the threshold and shrugs the duffel to the floor (2.0). He toes off his shoes heel against toe onto the mat, and they land askew (2.0). He asks for the bathroom and walks to wall B (3.0). She crouches and sets the shoes side by side, toes to the door, 2 cm apart (2.5); lays the duffel flat beside the mat (1.0); stands, steps back and looks (0.8); goes to the kitchen run.
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: his eyes on the surfaces, and he starts to ask. Trigger: 'Mind the step', then the white room. After: no more questions, only a joke and a request for the bathroom. Venus holds; her plan is running.
- **transition in**: Hard cut from the dark cabin to the white marble.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: the bathroom door closes, seen from the living room, and SC11 picks it up from inside as it clicks shut.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her step back carries it: after squaring PR-41 on PR-40 she steps back to her phone distance and looks at his black shoes in her white doorway (0.8), with nothing in her hands. Cut it and the viewer loses her framing him into her home as if through a lens, the composing that SC17's posed armchair and SC18's photograph finish.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Lobby and landing: no directory, names or numbers legible.; Shoes and duffel: no visible logos, tags or branded zip pulls (stylist and production designer confirm).

### SC11

- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on the tissue as he looks at the faint pink on it, drops it in the bowl and flushes. He wants to get back out to her fast. Real detail: he checks his teeth before he deals with the blood. He stops at the drops for half a second, then does quick, efficient housekeeping without a question. Through the door, ice drops into glasses; he grins and goes.
- **blocking**: LOC-09. JEAN comes in by the door (wall A) and goes to the mirror cabinet PR-49 above the basin (wall D). He crosses 1.4 m to the toilet PR-47 (wall C); the roll is on wall B at y 200; back to the basin tap, back to the toilet, and out. The seat is left up. Off screen, heard only as ice through the door, VENUS makes the set for SC12: jacket onto PR-62; PR-01 from her chest pocket face down on PR-53; PR-03 closed onto PR-42 at x 175, y 535, hinge south; PR-30 from her left jacket pocket into the ice of her tumbler. She changes into the T-shirt and fills both tumblers from PR-59.
- **action progression**: At the mirror: grin and teeth check (3.0). To the toilet (1.5); lifts the seat (0.5); sees the drops and stops (0.5). Tears off three sheets (1.0); wets them at the basin (1.5); wipes the rim (1.5); looks at the pink smear (1.5); drops the tissue in the bowl (0.5); flushes (0.5). Ice through the door; one more grin at the mirror (1.0); out.
- **emotional shift**: Before: grinning at himself. Trigger: the drops under the seat. After: a half-second stop, then he wipes them away and walks out smiling. He removes whatever doesn't fit the night he wants.
- **transition in**: Cut on action: the door clicks shut.
- **transition out**: Cut on action: he opens the bathroom door and SC12 picks him up stepping into the living room. The ice bridges the cut.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' SC11 carries plot, not the idea: the drops on PR-47 are the previous man's (A13), the first sign Jean is not the first man in this room. Cut it and the viewer loses that history and the pink tissue, but not the idea, which SC18 carries. Staged as written; blocker filed with the writer.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Dried drops. Method: practical makeup/SFX, 2-4 mm of #6E1C18 crust. Acceptance: after the wipe, a haze of 10 x 3 mm at most; no smear, run or spray. Risk: any more reads as gore.; Keep the tap beat: the wet wipe (PR-48 S2) is what makes the scripted pink physically true.; Mirror. Acceptance: only Jean is reflected.; The off-screen set is heard only (ice); nothing of it is shown, and nothing passes between people.

### SC12

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her fingertip as she slides the closed tin across, then lands it on the lid when he tries it. She wants to ask the last question first; JEAN wants the bedroom now. She plays it light and dry: 'You haven't shown me anything worth missing.' He downs his drink like a shot and blows out, breath only. The chimney straw stands in her ice: the last straw, standing again. She asks 'You feel like this space isn't safe?' warmly.
- **blocking**: LOC-08. Entry, set during SC11: jacket on PR-62, PR-01 on PR-53, PR-03 closed on PR-42 at x 175, y 535, hinge south, PR-30 standing in the ice of her tumbler. VENUS stands beside PR-44 at the table's east end, his tumbler in her right hand and hers in her left. JEAN walks from the bathroom door (wall B, y 120-200) along the sofa to its east end (y 540-590). He takes his glass from her right hand (practical) and sets it at x 140, y 490. She sits in PR-44 facing west, Jean on her right, knees about 35 cm from his. Her left hand sets her glass at x 175, y 520; her right fingertip slides PR-03 north to x 140, y 525. He turns it over and sets it back where it was, hinge south, seam north toward him; SC13's lid direction depends on this.
- **action progression**: He crosses to her (3.0). Lines. He takes his glass (0.8), downs it (1.5), blows out (1.0) and sets it down (0.5). She sits (1.5); her left hand sets her glass down with the straw standing (0.8); her right fingertip slides the tin across (1.0) and lets go. He picks it up and turns it over (1.2), sets it back at x 140, y 525, hinge south, seam toward him (0.5), and puts his thumbnail to the seam (0.3). Her right fingertip lands on the lid (0.3); he presses and it holds (0.8); he lets go, grins and sits back (1.0). Her fingertip stays on the lid to the end of the scene.
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: grabbing, at the drink and then the tin. Trigger: her fingertip holding the lid down. After: hands off, he sits back and waits for her. Venus holds: the fingertip on the lid is her state, and it doesn't change until his line in SC13.
- **transition in**: Cut on action from SC11's bathroom door opening.
- **transition out**: Hard cut in continuous time to SC13's wider shot as she begins 'Trust is a thing that is earned', her fingertip on the lid matching across the cut.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-30 standing in her ice and her fingertip holding PR-03 shut carry it: the picture with him is still possible if he tells her one true thing. Cut this and the viewer loses the last chance, and SC13's verdict has nothing to take away.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Blow-out. Acceptance: breath only; no liquid lands anywhere in the white room. Risk: a spray breaks the clean tone.; Straw in ice. Method: practical. Acceptance: it leans about 25° with its top about 10 cm above the rim, and stays up when the glass is set down.; Glass handed between people: practical only.; Tin orientation. Acceptance: at the end of the scene the hinge faces south and the seam faces Jean.; T-shirt: oversized team shirt with no logo or number (A10, stylist); no nudity.

### SC13

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hands as her left hand draws the straw from her ice and lays it flat, leaving a wet mark, her right fingertip lifts off the tin, and her right thumb opens it toward him. What's inside stays out of frame. She wants one true thing about love, his last straw; JEAN wants the bedroom. She asks her speech as a real question, warmly. His near hand goes to her thigh. While he reaches for a comeback, his eyes go to the tin twice. On 'That's for you to find out' he grins, thinking he has won. Her verdict lands with no change of face; only her hands move.
- **blocking**: Same marks as SC12. PR-03 at x 140, y 525, hinge south, seam toward Jean, her right fingertip on the lid. Jean leans in from the sofa's east end; his left hand is the near one and goes to her right thigh. Her left hand draws PR-30 from her tumbler (x 175, y 520) and lays it flat at x 160, y 488-508 along the table's long axis, leaving a 20 x 1.2 cm wet mark. Her right fingertip lifts, and her right thumb raises the lid from the north seam. It swings up about 100° on its south hinge, so the open well faces Jean and the lid's back faces south. The camera stays south of the raised lid and no higher than its top edge.
- **action progression**: Her speech (dialogue). He leans in (1.0) and puts his left hand on her right thigh (0.5). His speech, his eyes going to the tin twice. Her question; his line.
