The verdict: her left hand draws PR-30 out (1.0) and lays it flat (1.0); her right fingertip lifts (0.5); her right thumb raises the lid from the north seam (1.0). Hold on his face (2.0). Cut.
- **emotional shift**: Before: fingertip on the lid, straw standing, face open and warm, hoping. Trigger: 'That's for you to find out', a line instead of a truth. After: her hands lay the straw flat and open the tin while her face stays pleasant. The change is only in her hands.
- **transition in**: Hard cut in continuous time from SC12.
- **transition out**: Hard cut with an elision: from the open lid straight to SC14, with Jean already lifting her. What he takes from the tin, and how, stays off screen.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' PR-30 laid flat and PR-03 opening carry it: the hope of a picture with him alive goes down with the last straw, because he gave her a line. Cut this and the viewer loses why he dies, and that she wanted him to pass; SC18's photo would read as a trophy.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Tin interior. Acceptance: no frame ever shows into the well; the camera stays south of the raised lid and below its top edge. No contents, no preparation, no dosing (must avoid).; Wet mark on white gloss. Method: practical. Acceptance: it reads as a streak with no pooled liquid.; Hand on thigh: practical, with an intimacy coordinator.

### SC14

- **performance beats**: Jean's scene. Hero beat: close on his black-socked foot as it stops at the three drops, then steps over them. JEAN wants to get her to the bed; VENUS wants him to keep walking, and she never looks down. She turns his face back to hers with her right hand and says 'J.' It is the gesture she will repeat on him dead (V01-B07, SC18). Here, alive, he looks back.
- **blocking**: From the sofa's east end JEAN stands and lifts VENUS from PR-44. He carries her past the table's east end, with the open tin and the flat straw in view as they pass, south across the rug to the hall, then east to the bedroom door (wall C, x 540-630). PR-45 lies at x 575-600, y 880-886, just inside the threshold on the hall side. He stops with his lead foot short of the drops, then steps over into LOC-10.
- **action progression**: He stands (1.0) and lifts her (2.0). The carry, about 7 m (8.0). He looks down and stops for half a step (0.8). Her hand turns his face to hers (0.8); 'J.' (0.5). He steps over (1.0) and through the door (1.5).
- **emotional shift**: Jean's. Before: striding, eyes on her. Trigger: the drops at his feet. After: a half-step stop, eyes down. Second trigger: her hand on his jaw and 'J.' After: eyes up, he steps over and never looks back. Venus holds: she never looks down, because she doesn't know the drops are there.
- **transition in**: Hard cut with an elision from the open tin.
- **transition out**: Match cut: his black foot stepping over the drops on the white floor becomes his black shirt lying on the white bedroom floor just inside the door (x 200, y 60), later that night (SC15). The match is black on white at floor level.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her hand turning his face toward hers carries it: the last time he looks back. Cut this and the viewer loses the gesture's first, living use, so her turning his dead chin toward her (V01-B07) and toward the lens (SC18) has nothing to answer.
- **practical or compositing flags**: The carry. Method: practical, rehearsed with a stunt coordinator. Acceptance: steady, with no stumble.; Drops: 3-5 mm of dried crust that reads only in the close shot; no smear.

### SC15

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her hand as it passes her phone on the nightstand and turns up the speaker ring over his catching breath. She wants to stop hearing his breathing. Tactic 1: she folds his black clothes square. Tactic 2: the mirror cabinet, with the same face and hands as the padlock check. She lifts the organiser, opens one lid, looks, closes it and puts it back, with no wince and no pause on the turned label: the meds are seen and never given as the reason. Real detail: she shuts the mirror door with her eyes down and never meets her reflection. Tactic 3: she lies down. His breathing stops, then catches with a rasp, and her hand passes the phone to reach the speaker. JEAN: breathing only, face down, recorded wild.
- **blocking**: LOC-10. JEAN lies face down on the wall-D (south) side of PR-52; VENUS is on the wall-B (north) side. PR-56 is on the floor, the shirt at x 200, y 60 and the trousers at x 280, y 150; she folds them onto PR-55 (wall D, x 330-380, y 60-110). She goes out through the hall, passing the threshold drops without looking, to LOC-09: PR-50 on the middle shelf of PR-49, PR-51 on the top shelf, label to the wall. Back in bed she lies on her back, head to wall C, so her right side faces PR-53 (x 60-110, y 365-405). PR-01 lies face down nearer the bed (x 98, y 385) and PR-54 farther from it (x 75, y 385).
- **action progression**: She gathers the shirt (1.0) and the trousers (1.0), folds them (6.0) and sets them square on the chair, shirt on top (1.0). Walk to the bathroom (8.0). Opens the cabinet (0.8); lifts the organiser (1.0); opens row 1, column 1 (0.8); looks (1.5); closes it (0.5); puts it back (1.0); shuts the mirror door with her eyes down (0.8). Back to bed (8.0); lies down (2.0). His breathing runs slow and loud, stops for a gap (5.0), then rasps (1.0). Her right hand leaves the sheet (0.8), passes over the phone without slowing (0.5) and presses the speaker's dot, so the track plays low (0.5). She turns the ring to about 80% (2.0) and brings her hand back to her side (0.8). She lies still, eyes on the back of his head.
- **emotional shift**: Before: her hands stay busy with the folding and the cabinet. The organiser is a hold, with no trigger and no change, and the same face she gave the padlock. Trigger: his breath stops and catches with a rasp, which we hear. After: her hand goes past the phone to the music and turns it up until his breathing is under it. Then, for the first time, her hands go still.
- **transition in**: Match cut from SC14, black on white at floor level.
- **transition out**: Hard cut to morning with the track continuous: the same four bars loop across the cut, and the light on the same bed goes from night to morning.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her hand passing PR-01 for PR-54 carries it. The phone is her camera and the one thing that could call for help. Cut this and the viewer loses that she let him die while he could still be helped, so her waiting in SC16 and the photo in SC18 become love for a man she let go, not a collector's calm.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Meds. Acceptance: seen and never named; no legible label; all 28 compartments full. Risk: a close on the vial label or any diagnosis reads as the explanation (must avoid).; Breathing: wild sound, no lip-sync, face down.; Music: the original trance-soul track (A08), heard only from PR-54. Level changes come from her hand on the ring, not from the mix.; Mirror. Acceptance: no crew in the reflection, and she never meets her own. The speaker and the phone's back stay blank; no screen is lit or seen.

### SC16

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on his arm as she lifts it over her waist and it slides off. She wants him to turn over and reach for her. The look follows ref 06 as the brief describes it: he lies face down, mouth open; she lies awake with a hand to her forehead, eyes on the back of his head, not the ceiling. This is the one scene where her hands have nothing to do. Tactic 1: she waits through another loop of the track. Tactic 2: the arm. Tactic 3: 'J. Stay for breakfast.', at the same dry pitch as 'That's a drink', not pleading. Then she switches the music off. JEAN: absolute stillness, no breath.
- **blocking**: PR-52 rumpled. JEAN lies face down on the south side, his right arm off the bed on that side, his left arm toward her. VENUS on the north side, on her back. PR-54 still looping at about 80%, with PR-01 face down beside it on PR-53.
- **action progression**: Hold through a full loop (10.0); nothing moves but her breathing. She rolls onto her left side toward him (1.0), takes his left wrist in her right hand (0.5) and lifts his arm over her waist (1.5). She lets go and it slides off onto the sheet (1.0). Her line (2.0). She waits (3.0). She rolls back (0.8); her right hand goes to the speaker's dot and switches it off (1.0). Silence (hold 4.0).
- **emotional shift**: Before: still and waiting, a hand at her forehead. Triggers: his arm slides off her waist, which we see, and her line gets no answer, which we hear. After: she ends the music and the room goes silent. She stops waiting and gets up.
- **transition in**: The music bridges the cut from SC15.
- **transition out**: Hard cut on the silence to SC17's daylight kerb insert, the silence carrying across.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' His arm lifted over her waist carries it: she stages the picture of a couple waking up together, and it slides off. Cut this and the viewer loses the want at its plainest, feelings returned rather than sex (limerence), and the picture she can't make even here.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Jean's stillness. Method: practical breath-hold takes, or VFX paint-out of breathing. Acceptance: no rise in the chest across the take. Risk: one breath reads alive.; Arm slide. Method: practical, dead weight. Acceptance: it slides off rather than dropping stiffly.; Ref 06 is a look only, with no likeness; the bedding is white, not patterned, because the colour theme wins.

### SC17

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on the cloth as she scrubs out the three drops she missed until it is pink and the floor is white, then folds it square before she bins it. She wants the apartment spotless before she takes him away. She dresses him unhurried, with the same care she gave his collar in the car, never effortful: no strain, no struggle, his limbs going where her hands put them. The threshold is the only place she speeds up. Real detail: the pink cloth folded into a 10 x 10 cm square before it goes in the bin. The last image holds on the finished room longer than is comfortable: JEAN in black in the white armchair, sunglasses on, hands on the arms. From here the head rule holds: his head stays where she sets it.
- **blocking**: Insert: LOC-07 street frontage in daylight; LOC-01 top down, clean, passenger seat empty, 30 cm off the kerb at x 260-660, facing west; nobody in frame. LOC-10: she dresses him on the bed from PR-56 on PR-55, with PR-41 from PR-40. Cut: he sits in PR-44 (x 110-190, y 555-635, facing west), hands on the arms. She takes PR-04 from PR-05's outside pocket and puts it on him with both hands. She presses PR-05's zip shut and stands it on end at x 160-188, y 5-33. Bedroom: PR-01 off PR-53 into her pocket; she strips and remakes PR-52. At the threshold (PR-45, x 575-600, y 880-886) she kneels on the bedroom side, facing west. The cloth goes into PR-60 (x 650-680, y 470-500). Tumblers and bucket go to the kitchen run. She closes PR-03 at x 140, y 525 from its south (hinge) side, behind the raised lid, and puts it with PR-30 into the left pocket of the jacket on PR-62; she wipes PR-42. She is out of frame for the final hold.
- **action progression**: Insert (4.0, silent), nothing moving. Dressing, about 20 s of screen time cut into the action: shirt on, buttons, shoes, all at an unhurried pace. Hard cut: he is seated in the armchair, the move elided. PR-04 out of the duffel pocket (1.5) and on him with both hands (1.5). Zip pressed shut (1.5); duffel stood on end by the door (1.0). PR-01 into her pocket (1.0). She strips the bed (6.0), pulls the four fitted corners tight (8.0), and puts the duvet on and smooths it until no crease is left (6.0). Leaving, she looks down and stops (1.0); kneels (1.0); scrubs, faster (6.0); cloth pink, floor white (hold 1.0). Folds the cloth square (1.5); to the bin, pedal, drop, soft-close (4.0). Tumblers and bucket to the kitchen, washed off screen (3.0). Her right hand pushes the raised lid shut from behind it (0.8); tin and straw into the jacket's left pocket (2.0). She wipes the table, and the straw's wet mark goes with it (2.0). Hold on the room (10.0).
- **emotional shift**: Before: calm, methodical, never looking down. Trigger: the three drops at the threshold she had missed, the first time she looks down there. After: she kneels at once and scrubs harder and faster than at anything else, then folds the cloth square, back in control.
- **transition in**: Hard cut on SC16's silence to the daylight kerb insert, the silence carrying; hard cut upstairs to her hands at his shirt buttons.
- **transition out**: Match cut from Jean seated in black in the white armchair, sunglasses on (SC17's hold), to Jean seated in black in PR-19 under his wall by lantern light, sunglasses off (SC18). The white room's silence becomes the wind at the motel door.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Jean posed in PR-44 carries it: dressed, sunglasses on, at the centre of her spotless room, a portrait she makes of him and stays out of. Cut this and the viewer loses where the cold open's sunglasses and pose come from, and that she composes him for a picture rather than disposing of a body.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Closing the tin. Acceptance: the camera stays south of PR-03 and her hand closes the lid from behind it; no frame shows into the well (must avoid).; Dressing and moving the dead-weight actor: practical, with a body double, cut into and elided, played unhurried with no struggle shown. Acceptance: no visible effort beat and no head movement except under her hands.; Pink cloth. Acceptance: pink (#E3A9A6), not red, with no drips; the scrubbed patch reads flatter and whiter than the satin floor.; Kerb insert. Acceptance: the car matches LOC-01 (no badges, blank plate); no facade text, numerals or signage.; Final hold: 8-10 s with nothing moving; Jean's stillness tested as in SC16.; Taste note broken on purpose, per the world bible: no dish rack and no charging cable, because the tidiness is the horror.

### SC18

- **performance beats**: Venus's scene. Hero beat: close on her right thumb as it rests on the flip-camera icon and this time presses, and the screen shows both of them. She wants to get into one photograph with him. Her thumb rests as it did twice in the car. Trigger: she lifts her head from his shoulder and looks round, past the back of his head, at the next man's two prints she has just pinned, and holds: the wall has already moved on. She turns back, lays her head on his shoulder again and presses. For the first time her eyes go into a lens, her own front camera, and she is smiling. Her left fingertips push up under his chin toward the lens. It doesn't give, and she doesn't push harder or lower the phone. She takes the photo anyway with no disappointment played; the smile holds. JEAN: head rule; his face stays where she set it at dusk, eyes open, no blink.
- **blocking**: LOC-04, continuous from V01-B14. Jean was brought in off screen: he sits in PR-19 (x 210, y 420, facing south), face bare, eyes open, head still 60° to his own right, so his level gaze runs at bearing about 240°, toward her side. VENUS unhooks PR-04, folds it and sets it lenses-up at x 200-214, y 365-370, on his side of the oranges. She lays PR-02 on its side at the table's south-west corner (x 124-136, y 331-348), muzzle to the door, away from the chairs. From PR-06 under the window (x 95-140, y 20-45) she takes PR-28 and pins them in the paler rectangle on wall C at x 305-315, heights 170-185 and 188-203. She moves PR-18 to x 165, seat edge touching PR-19's, and sits on his west side (his right), head on his right shoulder; her left hand is the one beside him. Her right hand holds PR-01 at arm's length, about 65 cm out on bearing about 235°: close to his gaze line but raised 25-30 cm above his eye line and tilted down, front lens toward them, wall C behind. She never lowers it, so his level gaze passes under the lens. Trigger look: she lifts her head and turns over her left shoulder, past the back of his head (which faces north-east), to PR-28, then back. She sets the phone face up beside PR-25 (x 145, y 340), which ejects PR-27 south. She pins it at x 290-295.4, heights 165-173.6, between Jean's photos and PR-28, and sits back down beside him.
- **action progression**: Unhook PR-04 (1.0), fold (1.0), set down (0.8). Draw PR-02 from her back pocket (0.8), lay it down (0.8). To PR-06 (3.0); PR-28 out (2.0); to wall C (3.0); pins both (3.0). Moves PR-18 (1.5); sits (1.0); head on his shoulder (1.0). PR-01 out of her right front pocket (1.0), raised above his eye line (1.0). Thumb to the icon (0.8); rests (1.2). She lifts her head and turns to PR-28 (1.0); holds (1.0); back to his shoulder (1.0). Presses (0.3): the screen shows both. Left fingertips under his chin push up (0.8); nothing moves; she lets go (0.4). Shutter (0.3). Phone face up by the printer (1.0). Whir and eject (5.0). Card from the slot (1.0); she stands and pins it (3.0); sits back down beside him (2.0). The key actions sum to 39.7 s; the rest of the 2:00 is the final hold.
- **emotional shift**: Before: her thumb rests on the icon (1.2 s), as it did twice in the car. Trigger, which the camera sees: she lifts her head from his shoulder, turns past the back of his head to the next man's two prints, and holds 1.0 s. After: she turns back, lays her head on his shoulder again and presses. The press is the change: her own face on her screen, eyes in the lens, smiling. Second beat, a hold: his chin doesn't give, his gaze still passes under the lens, and she takes the picture with no change of face.
- **transition in**: Match cut from SC17: seated in black in the white chair becomes seated in black under the wall, with the wind at the door.
- **transition out**: End of the pilot. Hold on the wall (Jean's photos, the one card-size print of the two of them, the next man's two prints) with the two of them at the table below. Hard cut to black after the printer falls silent; the wind carries over the black.
- **campaign idea link**: 'She isn't collecting trophies; she's trying to get into one photograph with him.' Her first press of the flip icon, and PR-27 pinned between Jean's wall and the next man's, carry it. Cut this and the viewer loses the answer to every thumb rest: she wanted one picture with him and gets it only when he can't look. They also lose the setup the series repeats.
- **practical or compositing flags**: Front-camera screen. Method: composite the UI. Acceptance: the lantern lights her face, her eyes are in the lens, and his gaze passes visibly under it.; Phone height. Acceptance: in the front-camera frame the lens sits above his eye line, so his eyes read level and below it, never into it. If a take shows his eyes meeting the lens, raise the phone; never re-pose his head, which stays where she set it at dusk (bearing about 240° seated).; Chin push. Method: practical, stillness-trained body double. Acceptance: two fingertips press upward for 0.8 s and his head does not move by a visible amount. Risk: a nod reads alive.; Trigger look. Acceptance: her head leaves his shoulder, turns past the back of his head toward wall C, holds 1.0 s and returns before the press; it reads in the wide with the sound off.; PR-27. Method: a practical card pre-printed from the actual frame. Acceptance: with the sound off, it reads as the only card-size print on the wall.; PR-02 on the table. Acceptance: it stays out of the front-camera frame (DP confirms).; Dead eyes: the same makeup lock as V01, no blink, lids at the top of the iris.; PR-28: a fictional man; the courthouse facade has no inscription or seal, and no real case is shown. The printer's whir is the only non-wind sound; no score.

Absent: birds, insects and any other engine. This is the first clean, quiet sound in V01, and its cleanness against the phone's clipping is what the 35mm switch sounds like. | SND-D01-004 | D01 | foley + atmos + production dialogue (decorum at the bar) | Pilot SC05-SC08, about 180-660 s (3:00-11:00); 'Have we met before?' at the top of SC05 (about 180 s); the first verdict inside SC06 (about 330-480 s); SC08 window section from about 585 s; picture-led | You hear the game on the counter, and nothing scores it (A08: no jukebox, no bar music). VENUS: "Have we met before?" opens it over the bare room. VENUS: "How about I get this first round. Two Naked and Famous." Two glasses land together. A straw laid flat in the wet ring makes a soft, tacky tick; a straw drawn back out makes a dry slide; a glass is set down for every drink, so each lost straw has its own sound. SC06: VENUS: "A true martyr on a Thursday night, alone in an empty bar. Who slayed you, my friend?" JEAN: "She left. Packed while I was at work. I never once raised my voice." Her glass goes down, then the dry slide of the wall straw, then JEAN: "That's a no?" VENUS: "That's a drink." SC07: the chimney straw drags faintly as it is pushed into the soft wax. SC08, through the window: the bar goes silent behind the glass, and we hear only the street side, a far car and wind at the door frame. The laughs are seen, not heard, and the cuts land on them. At last call the room comes back. The room is a near-empty Thursday bar, carried by a drinks-fridge compressor and a glass washer, with any background voices as unintelligible walla. Dialogue is recorded clean on set. Cut on the laughs (tone: genuine). | Source: foley recorded for this production, and production dialogue recorded on set with the Venus, Jean and Bartender actors, whose written consent for the pilot scope is required. Walla is recorded with consented background performers and kept unintelligible. 'Naked and Famous' is a spoken cocktail name; the producer's check is pending (A10). Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-005 | D01 | foley (the tin and the last straw) | Pilot SC12-SC13, about 900-1200 s (15:00-20:00); the lid opens at the end of SC13, about 1195 s, the Act Two button; picture-led | No music in either scene (A08). SC12: her glass is set down on the white lacquered table, the straw standing in its ice. The closed metal tin slides across to him in one continuous glide of metal on lacquer, a low even hiss. He turns it over in his palm, and nothing inside is heard. He tries the lid: it lifts a hair and clicks back shut under her fingertip, one dry metal click. Then VENUS: "Let's take a trip." Now and then between lines, never under a word, the ice settles around the standing straw. SC13: after JEAN: "That's for you to find out." the straw comes out of the ice with a wet drag and is laid flat with a wet tick. Her fingertip lifts, the catch releases, and the lid swings open toward him: a dry hinge, and a small knock at its stop. Hard cut on the knock. Nothing inside the tin is ever heard; the method stays off screen. | Source: foley recorded for this production (a prop tin with a hinged lid, a white lacquered table, glass, ice, straw). Dialogue is covered by the Venus and Jean actors' pilot consent. Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-002 | D01 | voice, non-verbal + foley (Jean's breathing; the volume ring) | Pilot SC15, about 1290-1440 s (21:30-24:00); rasp about 1400 s; track entry about 1410 s; ring turn about 1415-1425 s; picture-led | His breathing is Jean's last sound: slow and loud, face-down, muffled by the pillow. Her side of the room: his black clothes folded square (cotton on cotton), the magnetic catch of the mirror cabinet, and one lid of the pill organiser snapped open and shut, with the tablets shifting once. No label is read and no drug is named. Back in bed, his breathing stops for a long gap, 6-8 s of room tone with nothing in it, then catches with one rasp on the inhale. No gurgle, no death-rattle cliché. That gap is the quietest point of the night. The track goes on low (SND-D01-001), and he breathes on in its holes. Then her fingers turn the speaker's wool-wrapped volume ring in one continuous rotation, from about 20% to about 80%: a soft wool rub over a faint, even felt-and-plastic detent. The track rises in step with her hand until his breathing sits under the pad. | Source: recorded for this production with the actor cast as Jean (not yet cast). His written consent is required for his breathing and the rasp, in the pilot scope (as SND-D01-001). No library or synthetic breath. The foley, including the ring on the PR-54 prop speaker, is owned by the production. Status: required, pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-001 | D01 | music, in scene (the trance-soul track) | Pilot SC15-SC16: entry about 1410 s (23:30), after the rasp; exit at her switch-off, about 1525 s (25:25); the line in a bar-4 hole inside SC16 (1440-1530 s); picture-led, moves with the pilot edit | The pilot's only music (A08). Venus plays it from the small white speaker on the nightstand to quiet herself; it is never score. It is heard from the bed, from a small mono speaker with little below about 120 Hz. Composer brief: 100 BPM, 4/4 (beat 0.6 s, bar 2.4 s), one four-bar loop of 9.6 s that repeats with no click, gap or tail at the join, written for the speaker's range. Bars 1-3: a soft four-on-the-floor kick whose attack carries the pulse, an off-beat bass pitched so its upper harmonics carry, electric-piano minor-ninth chords, a filtered pad and a low, wordless female vocal. Bar 4 (7.2-9.6 s of each loop) is the hole: kick, bass and piano drop out, leaving the pad, with its filter closed down, and one held low vocal note, so 1-4 kHz is nearly empty for 2.4 s. Entry: the play-dot click, with the loop starting low on a bar-1 downbeat. SC15: at low volume his breathing comes through each hole. She turns the ring from about 20% to about 80% (SND-D01-002), and the track rises with her hand until the pad and the held note cover him. SC16: the same four bars come round, and now each hole carries no breath. VENUS: "J. Stay for breakfast." sits in one hole: 4 words in about 1.6 s of its 2.4 s. Read: said to him, landing on 'Stay'; she is coaxing him up; not a plea, not grief. The music is laid in post, not played back on set, so the line records clean. Exit: she switches it off with a click, and the track stops hard mid-bar with no ring-out. Swap test: the hole is built for his breath, so on another film it lands on nothing. | Source: a commissioned original composition and master, with a wordless session vocalist. Scope: the pilot in all media, worldwide, in perpetuity, plus trailer, pitch and promo use of the pilot. Required: the composer's originality warranty and a similarity check, and the vocalist's written consent for that scope. No generated vocals or stems. The line is covered by the Venus actor's consent (SND-V01-015). Status: pending. Owner: producer. Not used in V01 or V02. |
| SND-D01-006 | D01 | foley + room tone (the clean-up) | Pilot SC17, about 1530-1680 s (25:30-28:00); silent kerb insert about 1530-1534 s; picture-led | No music. The switch-off at the end of SC16 leaves the room silent, and the 4 s daylight insert of the two-seater at the kerb plays in that same silence, as the script writes it: no street, no traffic. The quiet she made carries outside. Room tone comes in hard on the cut back upstairs: sealed and white, with a faint hiss from an air vent. Absent: the street, the neighbours and any clock. Dressing him while his arms still bend: black cotton drawn over his arms, the white armchair's cushion sighing under his weight, and the acetate hinge of his sunglasses opened and slid on (the V01 acetate take, a plant for SC01). His duffel zipped shut in one pull and stood by the door. The bed: old sheets dragged off, the fitted sheet's elastic snapping off each corner, one fresh sheet snapped open in the air, the corners pulled tight and tucked, her palms smoothing. The threshold: her knees on the hard floor, the damp cloth scrubbing the three drops in short hard strokes, two soft folds as the pink cloth goes square, then the bin's pedal and lid. The tin's hinge and a click as it shuts, and fabric as it goes into her pocket with the flat straw. Then the hold on the finished room with Jean in the white chair: room tone only, longer than is comfortable (tone: clean). Jean makes no sound. | Source: foley recorded for this production. The sunglasses come from the V01 acetate session, and the room tone is recorded in a white-finished interior (location release required). Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. Status: pending. Owner: producer. |
| SND-D01-003 | D01 | foley + sfx (the tag: the flip-camera press) | Pilot SC18, about 1680-1800 s (28:00-30:00); flip-camera press about 1750 s; picture-led | This follows straight on from V01, with the same motel room tone and wind (SND-V01-012 and 013). An acetate tick as she unhooks his sunglasses from her neckline and sets them by the oranges. Two push pins pressed into the wall for the new man's two prints. Cotton as her head settles on his shoulder, and the phone lifted. The focal sound, ahead of the printer: her thumb squeaks to the flip-camera icon and knocks in the phone body. These are the same takes as SND-V01-004 and 008, reused so the ear knows the gesture, and this time there is no slide back: the press completes. The flip is heard as a 2-frame room-tone dropout. Two fingers press his chin toward the lens: a faint press of skin on stubble, and then nothing. No creak, no collar and no chair, because the chin doesn't give. The photo is taken with no shutter sound (A10). Then the palm-sized printer whirs and the paper feeds out, the only sound in the room. One more pin as the print goes up between the two walls. Then room tone and wind, echoing V01's held frame. Proposal, for approval: no laugh here. Every laugh so far was aimed at him from behind the lens; in front of it she only smiles. | Source: foley recorded for this production. The thumb takes are reused from the SND-V01-004 and 008 sessions, the printer is recorded on a debadged unit, and the room tone is reused from the V01 session. Scope: the pilot, as SND-D01-001; owned by the production. No manufacturer UI sounds (A10). Status: pending. Owner: producer. |

_The storyboard predates the current shot plan and is rebuilt on the next run._

### Every shot in full

#### V01-SH01

- **scene id**: SC01
- **deliverable ids**: V01
- **purpose**: V01-B01 (0-4 s): he rides beside her with his head turned to the door, and her laugh shakes the picture · must-have. The phone UI is composited in finishing.
- **duration frames**: 96
- **fps**: 24
- **character ids**: JEAN; VENUS
- **look ids**: JEAN_L02; VENUS_L03
- **location id**: LOC-01
- **prop ids**: PR-01; PR-04; PR-56; PR-57; PR-11
- **framing**: FLOOR PLAN (LOC-01 driven through LOC-02): the car heads north in the northbound lane, and the axis is the road. C1 is Venus in the driver seat (west). C2 is Jean in the passenger seat (east), cheek on the headrest, face to the door (east). The sun is low in the WSW, behind the driver. PR-01 is in her right hand at arm's length, just west of his left shoulder, 120 cm above the road and level with his jaw, lens aimed east. Every V01 film setup in SC01 is west of the centreline or on it (the S01 still is declared separately). PICTURE: the phone's upright 9:16 frame runs full height, centred, and takes 31.6% of the 16:9 width, with a film-black (#0A0A0A) pillar of 34.2% on each side. Inside it is a CU of his black collar, left jaw, ear, one sunglasses arm and the neck mole. The jaw sits 40% down the phone picture and the collar fills its lower third. The east storefronts slide from frame-left to frame-right behind him.
- **lens intent**: Phone rear camera, 26mm FF-eq at 0.6 m, which sets the perspective, with 2x in-phone digital zoom (52mm-eq crop, field about 0.24 x 0.42 m). This in-world lens is outside the house set on purpose. Phone deep focus: collar, jaw and storefronts are all sharp, with slight softness and noise from the 2x crop.

Two of him, seen two ways, from two sides, and none of her · must-have. It carries V01's visual arc in one still. It breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose (decided upstream): a poster needs its lead. LIMERENCE only, in the top 15%. Declared key-art cheat: the phone is angled about 40° off its true aim so its screen faces the lens; the aim itself never shows and the picture is composited.

Venus sits at 1.84 m, about 22% across and 35% down, her head turned to Jean and tipped back in the laugh. Her nose points about 20° past him toward the lens, so the lens is about 48° off her nose: a three-quarter on her right side with the far iris clear of the bridge, her eyes on him and never on the lens. The phone screen, at 1.9 m, is about 60% across and 38% down, roughly 8% of the frame height. The real Jean, at 1.75 m, is about 78% across and 40-62% down: the back of his head above the headrest, his collar at the nape and the right sunglasses arm, head to the door. The horizon sits about 29% down, and sky and desert fill the top 15% for the title.

The front of her face is in shade, filled by bounce off the bone leather and her white shirt, 3:1. On the real Jean, the same sun rims his hair and his collar at the nape: film texture, gold halation on those edges, and highlights that roll off over his grey-ash skin. THE SCREEN is a plain lit panel in the plate. In finishing it gets V01's SH01 frame in phone look: 2x collar and jaw under the REC dot, deep focus, the sky clipped to flat white with no roll-off, compression blocks, the fingerprint smear hazing the stucco, and grain only. The timer is left off so LIMERENCE stays the only text. It is held 1 stop under her hair rim and below the halation threshold, so neither the screen nor the REC dot blooms.

Two of him, seen two ways, and none of her. LIMERENCE sits in the top 15% of sky. It breaks 'hands before faces' on purpose (decided upstream: a poster needs its lead).

- **board note**: The held frame from the doorway: a wall of one man, a table set for two, two empty chairs. At deck size every print must read as him alone, and the bare pine at centre-right is the space the ending fills. No text.
- **sound cues**: none: still, no audio
- **entry state**: No character in frame. PR-16: lit at x 180, y 365. PR-18 and PR-19: at the table, facing south, empty. Table: PR-21 six cans in two stacks, PR-22 two full jugs, PR-24 five oranges, PR-25 slot to the door. PR-20: about 250 prints of Jean alone, bare pine empty. PR-26 bedroll along wall B; PR-23 nine emptied jugs along wall D. PR-15: open, blue spill on the floor.
- **exit state**: Still, one moment: no character; PR-16 lit; PR-18 and PR-19 at the table, empty; six cans, two jugs, five oranges and PR-25 on the table; PR-20 about 250 prints, bare pine empty; PR-26 and PR-23 in place; PR-15 open, spill on the floor.
- **generation risk**: high: about 250 prints of one consistent fictional face, none of them together, no text anywhere. Method: a practical print wall or composited approved stills, pulled from the V01-SH25 clean plate and upscaled clean. 35mm check: S02's own film pass at master size; reject baked-in grain, bloom or blacks below #0A0A0A; only the lantern globe halates.

#### S03-SH01

- **scene id**: SC08
- **deliverable ids**: S03
- **purpose**: S03 social frame, 4:5: a straw house on the bar between two glasses, one roof straw being drawn away · must-have. Method: a practical tabletop shoot, because generated straws bend, multiply and cross. Exactly 5 straws in line plus 1 under her fingertip, with the base straw in its wet ring. No text.
- **duration frames**: 0
- **fps**: 24
- **character ids**: VENUS
- **look ids**: VENUS_L01
- **location id**: LOC-05
- **prop ids**: PR-29; PR-30; PR-31; PR-32
- **framing**: FLOOR PLAN (LOC-05; x east from wall B, y north, in cm): the counter runs along wall B at x 100-160, top at 107 cm. Jean is on stool 2 (y 350) and Venus on stool 3 (y 440). The house is centred at x 130, y 395, its roof apex to the west at x 111. The candle with the chimney straw is at x 112, y 418. His coupe is at x 140, y 350 and hers at x 140, y 440. The 4:5 frame is from the stool side, arm over the stool line: lens 225 cm off the floor (118 cm above the counter), 55 cm east of the house, looking west and down 65°, high so the flat house reads as a plan. The house sits at centre, about 35-66% down, with its roof pointing up-frame. Each coupe's inner rim sits just inside its side edge (his at left, hers at right), bowls cropped. Her fingertip enters from frame-right and draws one roof straw down-frame toward the near edge. The candle and chimney are top-right. The counter's far edge crosses about 27% down, with the soft lower back-bar shelves of blank bottles above it. The counter's worn front edge crosses about 78% down; below it the frame drops into soft dark between the stools. No cuff comes within 30 cm of the house.
- **lens intent**: 40mm FF-eq (28mm S35 prime), portrait 4:5 extraction (18.6 x 23.25 mm inside the S35 4-perf gate, camera rolled), at 1.3 m, T2.8: field about 0.86 x 1.08 m. It holds about 1.17-1.46 m, covering the house's roughly 16 cm of depth along the lens axis and her fingertip. The coupe bowls soften at the edges and the back bar is fully soft.
- **camera movement**: Still.
- **lighting**: FL-CANDLE (balance 3200K). Key: the candle flame, about 1900K (reading deep amber), just beyond the house at top right, backlights the straws, so each carries a warm edge and throws a short shadow toward the bottom left; the drawn roof straw, nearest the flame, is the brightest detail. The red neon strip (saturated red, 210 cm up on wall B) spills down the back-bar shelves at the top of frame. The wall-D sconces behind camera, about 2400K, fill softly, 8:1. The white straws separate from the dark counter by value, 12:1. The flame and coupe rims halate.
- **action**: Her fingertip draws the roof straw out of line.
- **performance**: A light, playful pull: a verdict passed off as flirting.
- **board note**: A top-down plan of a straw house between two coupes: five straws in line and her fingertip drawing one roof straw toward the near edge, with the chimney standing in candle wax at top right. With the sound off it reads as a game, and something being taken away. No text.
- **sound cues**: none: still, no audio
- **entry state**: VENUS (look L01): fingertip mid-draw on one roof straw, cuff more than 30 cm from the house. PR-29: 5 straws in line, base in PR-33's ring (flag 9), and 1 roof straw under her fingertip, part-drawn toward the near edge. PR-30: upright in PR-31's wax at x 112, y 418. PR-31: lit. PR-32: both coupes low, his at left, hers at right.
- **exit state**: Still, one moment: VENUS's fingertip mid-draw on the roof straw; PR-29 5 in line plus 1 under her fingertip, base in the ring; PR-30 upright in the wax; PR-31 lit; PR-32 both coupes low.
- **generation risk**: high if generated (straws bend, multiply and cross). Method: a practical tabletop shoot, as planned: exactly 5 in line plus 1 under her fingertip. 35mm check: S03's own film pass at its 4:5 master size; reject baked-in grain or bloom; the flame and coupe rims halate only in finishing; the dark between the stools stays above #0A0A0A.

### Contradictions the storyboard flagged

- 1 · V01-SH01, SH02, SH04-SH09, SH11-SH17, SH19-SH24 · Sound plan vs camera plan (owner: sound designer). Root cause: the V01 cues were spotted to the script's beat times, not the camera plan's shot splits, so picture-led hits land off their action (global frames). Laugh onset f12, but the jolt is at f43. Three collar strokes f134/f146/f158, but the picture has two strokes at f132-f161. Jump-cut roars and laugh snatches at f300/f360, but the cuts are at f302/f367. Thumb on f466-f480 and off f538-f550, but the picture has f451-f475 and f528-f547. Tap on the glass at f648 and light at f650, but the picture has a side-button press at f689 and light at f696. Perspective switch at f826, but the widen runs f835-f848. Laugh f864-f907 only, while she laughs on screen through SH12 (f835-f954) and SH13 (f955-f1007), V01's only sync shot, which needs peaks within 2 frames of the shakes. Headrest creak at f1070 and the eyes hold from f1092, but the chin turn runs f1080-f1114. Phone-mic return at f1238, but the narrowing is at f1248. Thumb on f1291-f1301 and off f1320-f1330, but the picture has f1282-f1301 and f1330-f1344. Pocket slide at f1416, but the picture seats it at f1419-f1440. Steps stop at f1507, after the tugs start at f1495 (the picture stops her at f1483-f1495). Bags f1565-f1579 and sacks f1596-f1632, but the picture has his bag f1555-f1582, her bag f1582-f1606 and the sacks f1606-f1632. One door thump at f1685, but the picture has two shoves. Lantern click f1738, but the picture click is f1747-f1762. Room order jug f1829, oranges f1853/f1862, printer f1891, chair f1920-f1949, but the picture order is oranges f1805-f1824, jugs f1824-f1843, printer f1848-f1862, chair f1874-f1908. Fix: re-spot every hit to the camera-plan frames above, and lay one continuous laugh under SH12-SH13 with sync peaks. Boarded with the plan's times, marked in each panel.
- 2 · V02-SH01-SH04, SH06-SH07 · The camera plan's re-cut in-points vs the sound plan's (owners: sound designer; cinematographer for SH07). SH01 picture is V01 5.5-7.0 s, but SND-V02-001 is cut from 6.5-8.0 s, so the stroke (f2) and collar press (f26) run 1.0 s out of sync; the pinch onset is at f29. SH02 picture is 15.3-17.8 s, the sound 15.0-17.5 s (0.3 s). SH03-SH04 picture is 42.8-43.8 s plus 45.0-46.0 s (a declared 1.2 s elision), but SND-V02-003 runs 42.8-44.8 s continuous, so its headrest creak (V01 44.6 s, before the turn) lands mid-turn at SH04 f19, and the sound plan expects the eyes first in the phone frame at 6.0 s while the picture lands them on film at 5.0 s. SH07 has no thumb slide-on (rest, back, mic cut), while script V02-B06 and SND-V02-006 (on at f269-f278) have one, so SH06's exit (no thumb) cuts to SH07's entry (thumb resting); the camera plan also gives the mic cut SH07's last 0.2 s (f307-f311), while SND-V02-006 cuts at f312. Fix: the sound re-cut follows the picture in-points (hook on the pinch at f29; creak on the turn at SH04 f0); the cinematographer re-splits SH07 as 0.4 s on, 0.8 s rest, 0.6 s back, 0.2 s cut, per the script, or the copywriter accepts the elision. Boarded as planned, with the elision marked.
- 3 · V01-SH09-SH17 and SH11, V02-SH01, V02-SH05-SH07 · Script cards vs camera-plan continuity (owner: copywriter, then finishing; cinematographer on plate A). (a) V01: B05 reads REC 00:58:40 at 26.0 s (SH09 f0), and the phone records unbroken through continuous action to SH16 f48 (52.0 s), which would then read 00:59:06, not B08's 01:04:55. Fix: B05 REC 01:04:29, running from SH09 f0. The SH08 to SH09 cut is already a time jump, so any value past 00:53:18 works; SH16 f48 then reads the script's own 01:04:55 unchanged, and SH17 ends on 01:04:59. (b) V01-SH11 shows the upright phone frame for 19 frames with no card, because B06's card is empty. Fix: the running timer continues, REC 01:04:37 at SH11 f0. (c) V02-B01's 00:06:12 sits on V01 footage 1.5 s into B02. Fix: V02-B01 REC 00:06:13. (d) V02-SH05-SH07 cannot be exact to V01: plate A's 3.0 s eyes hold would sit at V01 48.4-51.4 s, while SH15 has the phone face up in her lap. Re-planning plate A can't fix it either, since V02 plays the eyes before the laugh that V01-SH17 opens on. Proposal: declare V02's clock a teaser cheat. V02 keeps the script's 01:04:55, 01:04:58 and 01:05:00, which are consistent within V02 (SH06 f0 is SH05 f0 plus 3.0 s, SH07 f0 plus 5.0 s); the frames V02-SH06 f24-f47 share with V01-SH17 f0-f23 read 01:04:59 in V02 and 01:04:55 in V01. Boarded with the script's cards as written; SH16's card starts at f48.
- 4 · V01-SH02, SH07, SH14, SH15, SH17, V02-SH01, V02-SH07 · Cast bible vs camera plan (owner: character designer). VENUS's anchors put the scar for the collar and chin touches on her RIGHT hand and the flip-icon thumb nick on her LEFT thumb. The camera plan brushes the collar with her LEFT hand and uses her RIGHT thumb on the icon (right hand holds the phone, left drives), which fits the driving logic; the chin turn (SH14-SH15) is her right hand, so that anchor still reads. What breaks: the hand-double thumb plates and the collar insert carry no anchor, so identity checks fail. Fix: move the thumb nick to the right thumb and add a left-hand anchor for B02. Boarded to the camera plan.
- 5 · V01-SH21-SH23 · World bible vs camera plan (owner: production designer, with the cinematographer). PR-22 (two full jugs) rides loose in the boot, but SH21 carries only two bags and two sacks, and SH23 sets two jugs on the table. What breaks: props appear without being carried in, and the jugs stay in the open boot. Fix: pack both jugs in sack 2 under the oranges (15 x 15 cm each fits side by side in the sack's 30 x 18 cm base; 27 cm is under 43 cm), so the carry stays two bags and two sacks. Boarded as planned, marked.
- 6 · V01-SH17 to SH18 (time jump) · Camera plan and script vs world bible. (a) SH15 turns Jean's face to about 30 degrees 'in rigor', but script B11 and SH18/SH21 have his head back to the door, and nobody turns it. (b) PR-01 has no state from V01-B09 to SC18 and isn't in the motel frames (her right back pocket holds PR-02). Owners: writer (a) and production designer (b). Fix: (a) the writer chooses between an off-screen re-turn during the jump and keeping about 30 degrees in SH18/SH21 while amending B11; (b) PR-01 goes in her left front jeans pocket, recording stopped, from SH18. Boarded as head to the door, PR-01 unassigned.
- 7 · V01-SH23 · World bible vs camera plan (owner: production designer). PR-16's state reads 'switched on with a click and set at x 180, y 365', which can mean she carries it in and sets it down; but no bag or sack carries it (PR-07 holds cans, printer and oranges), and SH23 gives the click only 0.6 s, right after the sacks go down, in a frame that opens black. Not resolved quietly: boarded as standing at x 180, y 365 in the dark, unseen until the click, and marked. Fix: add a PR-16 state before V01-B12: on the table at x 180, y 365, off, left there when she last went out.
- 8 · V02-SH04 · Camera plan, internal (owner: cinematographer). The purpose says the chin turn 'stops with no loll', but the 45.0-46.0 s source covers only 1.0 s of V01-SH15's 1.4 s turn (f0-f34), so the head is still moving at the cut and never stops on screen. Fix: move the in-point to 45.4-46.4 s, so the last 1.0 s of the turn ends on the stop frame; the declared elision grows from 1.2 s to 1.6 s, and SND-V02-003 re-cuts with it. Boarded as planned: exit at about 70% of the turn.
- 9 · S03-SH01 · World bible and camera plan vs physics (owner: production designer). PR-29's SC08 state and S03 keep the base straw 'in its wet ring' at last call, but PR-33 left that ring in SC05, hours earlier, and was moved away then; a condensation ring would have dried. Fix: re-wet it (a glass set down on the base during SC08's window montage), or dress it as a dried ring in the counter's dull #5A4030, like the other rings. Boarded as given: 'in its wet ring'.
- Checks run: 37 panels, one per camera-plan shot and in its order (V01-SH01-SH26, V02-SH01-SH08, S01/S02/S03-SH01). Frame totals: V01 2160 f = 90 s; V02 360 f = 15 s. Cards and lines checked word for word against the script: no spoken lines (A15); LIMERENCE only on V01-SH26, V02-SH08 and S01; V01-SH16's card starts at f48, where the frame narrows. Every cue ID exists in the sound plan, and each hit was converted to shot-local frames and compared with the camera-plan action. Each entry equals the previous exit except the marked time jumps, elisions and scene changes: V01-SH02, SH04, SH05, SH06, SH07 (timer jump), SH09, SH18, SH20, SH21, SH23; V02-SH02, SH03, SH04 (the declared 1.2 s elision), SH05 (plate A, the teaser clock in flag 3), SH07 (the elided slide-on). Every tracked element has a written entry and exit for each panel it appears in, off-picture panels included. First looks of 1 s or more: V01-SH02 4.9 s, SH12 5 s, SH15 3.6 s after the turn, SH18 1.25 s, SH20 4 s, SH23 4 s; V02-SH01 1.5 s, SH03 and SH04 1.0 s. The 35mm look is checked per panel: film frames are generated clean, get the shared film layer in finishing, and reject baked-in texture or blacks below #0A0A0A; phone frames reject halation, bloom or shallow depth. V02 muted read: the timer, the shake, the thumb and the card carry it, so no caption is needed. Must-avoid list: no VO, slow motion, gore, nudity or brands; legible text is limited to STOP, MOTEL, OFFICE, the REC timers and LIMERENCE. Verified facts shown: the gun in her back pocket (SH19-SH24); the wall of him with none of them together (SH23-SH26, S02). No claim problem or clash with Lucas's notes was found, so there are no producer blockers.

- V01-SH04-IMG (V01-SH04): ASPECT fallback. No cast, so no reference is needed.

- V01-SH06-IMG (V01-SH06): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH07-IMG (V01-SH07): ASPECT fallback.
- V01-SH09-IMG (V01-SH09): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH10-IMG (V01-SH10): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH12-IMG-CAR (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH12-IMG-V (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH12-IMG-J (V01-SH12): ASPECT fallback. One reference. He stays frozen from this still, so he cannot breathe.
- V01-SH13-IMG (V01-SH13): ASPECT fallback. The composite is the one slot; he is only a soft black shape, so bleed is low.
- V01-SH14-IMG (V01-SH14): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH15-IMG (V01-SH15): ASPECT fallback. One reference; her fingers are described in words and checked at visual lock.
- V01-SH16-IMG-J (V01-SH16): ASPECT fallback. The two people are plated separately.
- V01-SH16-IMG-V (V01-SH16): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH17-IMG (V01-SH17): ASPECT fallback: the 576x1024 crop covers the 562x998 V02 card but needs a 1.25x upscale to feed a 720x1280 clip.
- V01-SH18-IMG (V01-SH18): ASPECT fallback. It wants the motel, the car and her; it sends the motel, and the small car and figures are described in words.
- V01-SH20-IMG (V01-SH20): ASPECT fallback. It wants her and LOC03-MOTEL@v1; it sends her (back view), and the office is described in words.
- V01-SH21-IMG (V01-SH21): ASPECT fallback. It sends CAR-01@v1, which fills the frame; her face is turned away and she is described in words.
- V01-SH22-IMG (V01-SH22): ASPECT fallback. It wants her and LOC03-MOTEL@v1; it sends her (back view), and the door and path are described in words.
- V01-SH23-IMG (V01-SH23): ASPECT fallback. No reference is sent, so his face cannot leak into the blank prints.
- V01-SH25-IMG (V01-SH25): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V01-SH24-IMG (V01-SH24): ASPECT fallback. One reference.
- V02-SH03-IMG (V02-SH03): ASPECT fallback. It is regenerated at 9:16 because a crop of a 720p frame would be 405 px wide. The practical glasses insert is reused.
- V02-SH04-IMG (V02-SH04): ASPECT fallback. It is regenerated at 9:16 in place of the crop.
- S01-IMG-V (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback: a 683x1024 crop, and the poster master needs the missing upscaler. It is approved at crop size.
- S01-IMG-J (S01-SH01): ASPECT fallback, with the same master limit as S01-IMG-V.
- S02-COMP (S02-SH01): The master size needs an upscaler, which the profile lacks.
- Missing capability: Image aspects other than square (16:9, 9:16, 2:3, 4:5) at clip-input or master size
- Missing capability: An image upscaler for clip inputs and the S01 and S02 masters
- Missing capability: Multi-reference input (a character plus a car or location in one job)
- Missing capability: Video clips longer than 5 s, including a 10 s option if that is what 'full 10s' means
- Missing capability: End-frame (first and last frame) conditioning for the chained clips and the SH15 and V02-SH04 reveals
- Missing capability: Video output above 720p, or a video upscaler, for the V01 master and the 1080x1920 V02 full-bleed shots

### Every job in full

#### K01-CHR-JEAN · V01-SH01

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet (JEAN_L02 dead state) -> CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **prompt**: Character reference sheet on a plain mid-grey backdrop showing one man of about forty in three views: front, left profile, and from just behind his left shoulder. Broad shoulders, thick neck, short wide face, square jaw, heavy low brows, close beard with a hard cheek line, dense tight black curls kept short at the sides, deep brown skin washed grey-ash at the lips and lids, and a small dark mole on the left side of his neck below the ear. Blue-black long-sleeve shirt buttoned to the top. Eyes open, fixed past the camera. Flat even light, everything sharp, no text.
- **acceptance checks**: Same face in all three views, with the mole on his LEFT neck in each (reject any mirrored view); Top button fastened; skin grey-ash but never lightened; no text
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square sheet, which fits the profile. Kit sheets use neutral light and no look sentence. Heritage is recorded but never prompted; Lucas confirms casting before lock.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K03-HND-VENUS · V01-SH02

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet (hands state reference) -> HND-VENUS@v1
- **prompt**: Hand reference sheet on a plain mid-grey card: a woman's right and left hands, shown backs up and then palms up. Long fingers, prominent knuckles, medium-deep brown skin with a warm undertone, bare short nails filed square. A pale two-centimetre scar across the back of the right hand between the thumb and index knuckles, and a pale nick across the back of the left thumb at its first joint. White shirt cuffs rolled above both wrists. Soft even light, everything sharp.
- **acceptance checks**: Five fingers on each hand, with no rings or polish; The scar and the nick sit on the hands flag 4 settles
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits the profile. Flag 4 is open: the character designer settles which hand carries which anchor before this locks. The hand double for SH02, SH07, SH14 and SH17 then matches it.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K02-CAR · V01-SH12

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet (LOC-01) -> CAR-01@v1
- **prompt**: Vehicle reference sheet of a vintage open two-seater roadster with a 1950s shape, in three views: three-quarter front, side and three-quarter rear. It has a long bonnet, rounded wings, low cut-down doors, a plain oval grille with six horizontal bars, round lamps, plain hubcaps and no ornament or badges. Silver-grey metallic paint, bone leather seats and headrests, black steering wheel, driver seat on the left, top down. Parked on pale gravel in flat overcast light, everything sharp, rear plate blank.
- **acceptance checks**: No badge, script or grille shape from a real make reads, and the plate is blank; Six-bar oval grille, bone headrests and left-hand drive in every view
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits the profile. It is the one reference sent to V01-SH12-IMG-CAR and V01-SH21-IMG.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K04-CHR-VENUS · V01-SH12

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit sheet (VENUS_L03, four views including a full-length back view) -> CHR-VENUS@v1
- **prompt**: Character reference sheet on a plain mid-grey backdrop showing one woman in her mid-thirties in four views: front, right three-quarter and left profile from the waist up, plus one full-length view from behind. Long face, narrow jaw, heavy straight brows with the right one slightly higher, three small dark freckles on the left cheekbone, open pores, fine laugh lines, medium-deep brown skin with a warm undertone, no makeup. Dense dark brown curls with honey highlights, gathered in one low puff at the nape. White tucked button shirt with the top two buttons open and the sleeves rolled twice. Pale-blue high-rise bootcut jeans, brown-black belt, brown-black ankle boots, right back pocket empty. Flat even light, everything sharp, no text.
- **acceptance checks**: Right brow higher and three freckles on the left cheek in every face view; reads 30+, with pores and laugh lines kept; Back view: low puff, tucked white shirt, pale-blue bootcut jeans, brown-black belt and boots, right back pocket empty
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Fits the profile. The back view carries her through SH19 to SH22, where her face is turned away. Heritage is not prompted; Lucas confirms before lock.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K05-JEAN-EYES · V01-SH15

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image state reference (dead eyes) -> JEAN-EYES@v1
- **prompt**: State reference: a close view of the same man's face in three-quarter from his left, eyes open, both upper lids resting exactly at the top edge of the dark brown irises. The corneas are dry and dull under a faint grey film, with a matte surface and no catchlight. Fine gold dust sits on the lashes, in the lid creases and in the forehead lines; the lips are dry and grey-ash. Broad soft light from frame-right, plain dark backdrop, everything sharp.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: No catchlight or wet shine, and both lids at the top of the iris; Far iris clear of the nose bridge; same face as CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square image with one reference, which fits.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K06-MOTEL · V01-SH18

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image kit view (LOC-03) -> LOC03-MOTEL@v1
- **prompt**: Abandoned 1950s roadside motel in flat desert at dusk: a two-storey bleached stucco room block with a gutted upper floor, a single-storey office with a boarded door, a long corrugated carport on steel posts, and three dry palms. A tall sign on steel legs carries a blank peeling cream box and a blank faded red arrow panel. Empty highway frontage, gravel forecourt, soft last light from the west, pale blue-grey sky, everything sharp, no lettering anywhere.
- **acceptance checks**: Sign faces blank, ready for the composited letters; no vehicles or people; Office left of the carport and room block behind, as in the LOC-03 plan
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Square location reference, which fits the profile.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### K07-PRINTS · V01-SH23

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image print set (PR-20 source) -> PRINTS-JEAN@v1
- **prompt**: Twelve separate candid photographs of the same man, each taken from far off with the background compressed close behind him: at night through a bar window, crossing a street at a daylight crosswalk, and standing at a plain fuel pump. He is alive, with warm healthy skin and his top button open, and wears all black in every picture. He is alone in every frame. Signs and storefronts are blank, with no text or numerals anywhere.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: He is alone in every print and none shows another person (verified fact); Same face in all 12; no text, numerals or logos
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: Each print is generated square and cropped to 3:2, which is enough at wall size. The only reference is the dead state, so the prompt states the living change. The roughly 250 wall prints are tiled from these 12.
- **estimated cost unit**: 12 x 2 x 3 = 72 images, price unknown

#### V01-SH01-IMG · V01-SH01

- **job type**: image
- **method**: text-to-image hero still, 9:16 phone picture (pillars and UI in finishing); also becomes the locked collar element for SH04 to SH08
- **prompt**: Upright phone-video frame, close on a man's black shirt collar, left jaw, ear and one black sunglasses arm. His head is turned away to the passenger door, cheek resting on a pale bone leather headrest. A fine pale-gold dust film lies over both shoulders and both collar points. Low late sun from behind the lens on its right rakes his jaw and collar gold, while his turned face sits in its own shade. Bleached blank storefronts behind, with a faint fingerprint haze over the brightest stucco. Phone-video look: deep focus with everything sharp, hard-clipped highlights, slight digital softness; clean image, grain added later.
- **reference asset ids**: CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1
- **acceptance checks**: Mole on his LEFT neck; only his jaw and ear show (reject mirrored); Collar C1: dust film on both shoulders and both points. Phone look: no bokeh or halation, storefronts unlettered
- **capability supported**: false
- **capability notes**: ASPECT fallback. It wants the man and CAR-01@v1; it sends the man and describes the headrest in words.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 x 2 x 3 = 6 images, price unknown

#### V01-SH01-VID · V01-SH01

- **job type**: video
- **method**: image-to-video from approved still STL-V01-SH01@v1; laugh jolt and rolling-shutter skew as 2D moves in finishing
- **prompt**: The storefronts behind him slide steadily from frame-left to frame-right for the whole clip, about one shopfront per second. A loose awning flaps once near the middle. His head, jaw, collar and sunglasses stay completely still, with no breath, blink or nod. The camera holds its angle; the laugh jolt is added later. Ambient wind and engine only.
- **reference asset ids**: STL-V01-SH01@v1
- **acceptance checks**: A difference matte shows no motion in his head and collar (freeze from frame 0 if it does); Background moves left to right without warping; exit collar still C1
- **capability supported**: true
- **capability notes**: (96+24)/24 = 5.0 s: one 5 s clip at 9:16 720p.
- **estimated cost unit**: 1 clip x 2 x 3 = 6 clips, price unknown

He faces his door. Beyond his head at upper left, about three metres off, a sun-faded red octagonal road sign with a blank face stands over an empty crossroads and a strip of rippled sand. Low gold sun from behind the lens on its right. Phone-video look: deep focus with everything sharp, hard-clipped highlights, slight digital softness; clean image, grain added later.

Only the hand, collar and shoulder show. Low warm light from behind the lens on its right. Phone-video look: deep focus with everything sharp, hard-clipped highlights, slight digital softness; clean image, grain added later.
