## Integrity check

Recommendation: revise.

| Severity | Where | Issue | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| major | Package-wide (user request) | The user wrote 'Camera department needs to be full 10s'. The storyboard reads this as 'a quality bar (10/10)', and the generation plan says 'If Lucas meant 10-second clips, the profile offers 5 s only'. The camera plan never addresses it, and nobody has confirmed the reading with Lucas. If he meant 10 s clips, the generation method, chaining and cost all change. If he meant 10/10, this audit still finds open camera defects (Q03, Q17, Q18, Q21), so camera sign-off cannot be given yet. | producer |
| major | V02-SH03, V02-SH04 (4.0-6.0 s) | The brief says V02 is 'rebuilt from V01 footage', and the camera plan makes V02-SH03 and SH04 '9:16 crop (1215x2160 or more) of the clean 4K 16:9 plate'. The generation plan instead has V02-SH03-IMG as a 'text-to-image native 9:16 reframe' and V02-SH04-IMG as 'regenerated at 9:16 in place of the crop'. The teaser's reveal face then becomes a separate generation from V01-SH15, and nothing checks that the two match. Fix: produce V01-SH14 and SH15 at 3840x2160 or better and crop them, or declare the deviation to Lucas and add an identity-match check against V01-SH15. | generation_supervisor |
| major | S01-SH01 (key art) | The brief asks S01 for 'Jean pale and still beside her in golden light'. The camera plan frames 'the back of his head above the headrest, his collar at the nape and the right sunglasses arm', and the screen insert is SH01's collar and jaw. The generation acceptance check for S01-IMG-J is 'His face never shows'. The style bible puts the pallor in the 'grey-ash wash at the lips, the lids and under the eyes', none of which reads from behind with sunglasses on, so the key art reads as a sleeping passenger. Fix: reframe so a grey-ash sliver of his right profile (jaw line, lips, ear) reads past the headrest in the gold light, with his eyes still behind the glasses. | cinematographer |
| major | S01-SH01 | S01-IMG-V sends only CHR-VENUS@v1 and S01-IMG-J sends only CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1. Neither sends CAR-01@v1, and unlike V01-SH12 (IMG-CAR + IMG-V + IMG-J) there is no car plate. Both prompts generate the car themselves ('from just behind an open two-seater's boot'), so two independently generated car bodies must be matted into one poster, and the car can drift from the V01 car. Fix: add an S01 car plate from CAR-01@v1 and register each person plate to it, as in SH12. | generation_supervisor |
| major | V01-SH12 to SH13 (34.8-42.0 s), plus SH02, SH07, SH09, SH14, SH15, SH19-SH24 | SND-V01-015 lays the B06 laugh only at 36.0-37.8 s (f864-f907). She laughs on screen through SH12 (f835-f954), and SH13 is 'the only sync in V01: the laugh's audio peaks land within 2 frames of the visible torso shakes', yet no laugh is spotted under SH13 at all. Other hits miss their action: three collar strokes (f134, f146, f158) against two strokes on picture (f132-f161); one door thump (f1685) against two shoves; the lantern click at f1738 against f1747-f1762 on picture; the pocket slide at f1416 against f1419-f1440; and the room foley in a different order from the picture (storyboard flag 1). Fix: re-spot every hit to the camera-plan frames and lay one continuous laugh under SH12-SH13 with its peaks on the shakes. | sound_designer |
| major | V02-SH01 (0-1.5 s), V02-SH04 (5-6 s), V02-SH07 (11-13 s) | SND-V02-001 is cut from V01 6.5-8.0 s, but V02-SH01's picture is V01 5.5-7.0 s. The stroke (f2) and the collar press (f26), which are the hook's sync sounds, therefore land 1.0 s off, while the pinch starts at f29. SND-V02-003 runs 42.8-44.8 s continuously, but the picture is 42.8-43.8 s plus 45.0-46.0 s, so the headrest creak lands mid-turn at SH04 f19. SND-V02-006 spots a thumb slide-on at f269-f278 that the picture does not contain (storyboard flag 2). Fix: re-cut every cue to the picture in-points. | sound_designer |
| major | V01-SH02, SH07, SH14, SH15, SH17; V02-SH01, SH07; job K03-HND-VENUS | Casting puts the right-hand scar on 'the anchor for the collar and chin touches (V01-B02, B07)' and the left-thumb nick 'where the thumb rests on the flip-camera icon'. The camera plan brushes the collar with her LEFT hand (SH02) and puts her RIGHT thumb on the icon (SH07, SH17). The director, style bible, storyboard (flag 4) and generation plan ('Settle the anchors before K03-HND-VENUS locks') have all raised it, and it is still open. The hand-double plates carry no working anchor. Fix: move the nick to the right thumb and decide whether B02's left hand needs an anchor. | casting_director |
| major | S01, V02, jobs K01 and K04 | Casting's blocker: 'Heritage for all four characters is a casting choice ... S01 and V02 make a dead Black man the key image beside a laughing woman, and they travel without D01's context', with 'Lucas approves or reassigns with S01 and V02 roughs in front of him'. No approval is recorded. The generation plan lists 'Lucas confirms casting before lock' for K01 and K04. This is a representation decision that only Lucas can make. | producer |
| major | D01 SC15 (21:30-24:00) | The must-avoid says the meds 'are seen, never named as the reason'. In SC15 the organiser beat (all 28 compartments full) is followed directly by her lying down, his breath stopping and rasping, and her hand passing the phone to turn the music up. Placing the meds straight before the climactic choice invites the reading that the missed meds caused it. The casting blocker ('Get human or sensitivity review of SC15 ... before the pitch pieces circulate') and H6 are both unrun. Fix: move the organiser beat away from the lead-in to the choice (for example into SC17's cleaning, or before SC12), and have the producer get the H6 sensitivity read before D01 is sent. | copywriter |
| major | V01-SH19 (57.25-60.0 s) | SH19 has only 'V01-SH19-PRAC ... previs still' ('Previs only'; cost 'plate shoot with the producer and armourer'), so the whole shot is practical. That needs a cabin matching CAR-01 (silver-grey dash, bone leather, a 30 x 14 cm drop-down glovebox), a Venus double in L03, a still Jean double, and the carport at last light with a remote head at 245 cm. The generation blocker books only 'a hand double, the armourer and a tabletop unit', so none of this is booked or costed. The treatment's preferred method was 'G, or a practical plate if the generator can't hold its shape'. Fix: generate the cabin plate from CAR-01 and composite a practical hand-and-replica insert shot on a matched dash buck, or scope and cost the full practical. | generation_supervisor |
| major | V01 master; V01-SH14, SH15, SH17 plates; V02; S01 and S02 masters | The camera plan requires 'Plates ... clean ... at 4K wherever V02 crops them', an SH17 'Plate plan, clean 4K', and a V01 master at 1920x1080. The generation plan's missing_capabilities include square-only 1024 px images, no image upscaler, and 'Video output above 720p, or a video upscaler, for the V01 master and the 1080x1920 V02 full-bleed shots'. It also notes that 1024x576 crops feeding 1280x720 clips need an upscale of 1.25x or more. As bound, V01 cannot reach its master resolution, V02 cannot be cropped from V01 (Q02), and the S01 and S02 masters 'need the missing upscaler'. Fix: specify an adapter or upscaler path that meets these outputs, or re-spec the masters with Lucas's approval. | generation_supervisor |
| major | V01, V02, S01 (phone UI, roadster); D01 SC05 and SC12 | Clearance is pending on the original phone UI (A10; the camera blocker says 'confirm clearance before any pitch piece circulates'), on 'Naked and Famous' (script: 'producer confirms whether the cocktail name needs clearance'), on the roadster reading as 'a real make's trade dress' (world bible blocker), and on the team T-shirt (style blocker: 'Lucas chooses'). 'Nothing is claimed as cleared', yet V01, V02 and S01 are the pieces that circulate. | producer |
| major | All sound cues (SND-V01-001 to SND-D01-006) | Every cue is 'Status: pending' or 'required, pending'. The Venus laugh, the only voice in V01 and V02, has a performer who is 'not yet cast', and her written consent must cover V01, V02, social, the deck and the pilot. Also pending: the composer's originality warranty and the vocalist's consent (SND-D01-001), consent for Jean's breathing (SND-D01-002), the vehicle-hire and location agreements (SND-V01-001), and the room-tone location releases (SND-V01-012, SND-D01-006). | producer |
| major | Generation plan (all jobs) | The generation blockers read 'No unit price for any image or clip; the worst case is 318 images and 192 clips' and 'No adapter is bound'. Generation cannot start without a spend ceiling from Lucas and a bound adapter. | producer |
| major | Strategy / D01 season question | The brief's key_message ('no man she hunts ever loves her back') contradicts A17, the season question D01 must carry. Strategy proposes 'Punishing the man the system lost can't make him love her back.' and is waiting on Lucas (strategy blocker 1). The proposition that D01 and the pitch rest on is therefore unapproved. | strategist |
| major | D01 SC04, SC06; proposition | Strategy blocker 4: 'A02 is unconfirmed. Whether Jean is guilty decides whether the man the system lost holds for the pilot.' The SC04 'NO FURTHER ACTION' report and the SC06 lie ('I never once raised my voice') both depend on it. Lucas has to confirm. | producer |
| minor | V01-SH03 (8.9-10.0 s) | The camera plan has 'the sign slides out frame-left'. The lens is aimed ENE (68°) from west of Jean, with the sign about 4.5 m east and 3 m north. As the car moves north, the sign's bearing rises from about 56° through 68° toward 90° and beyond, so it crosses the frame left to right and exits frame-right. The generation plan flagged this but prompted 'frame-left' anyway. Fix: correct the exit side (or state a phone pan) and update the V01-SH03-VID prompt. | cinematographer |
| minor | V02-SH07 (11-13 s), V02-SH04 (5-6 s) | V02-B06 scripts 'Her thumb slides onto the flip-camera icon ... rests ... then slides back'. The camera plan's SH07 is only 'rests (1.2), slides back (0.6), mic cut (0.2)', so the thumb pops in at the cut from SH06. V02-SH04's 45.0-46.0 s source covers only 1.0 s of the 1.4 s turn, so the head is still moving at the cut, yet the generation acceptance claims it 'lands on V01-SH15's end pose' (storyboard flags 2 and 8). Fix: re-split SH07 as 0.4 on, 0.8 rest, 0.6 back and 0.2 cut, and move SH04 to 45.4-46.4 s. | cinematographer |
| minor | V01-B05 to B08 (26-56 s), V01-SH11, V02-B01, V02-B04 to B06 | V01-B05 reads 'REC 00:58:40' at 26.0 s, and after 26 s of continuous, cut-on-action picture B08 reads 01:04:55 (it would be 00:59:06). SH11 shows the upright frame for 19 f with no card. V02-B01's 00:06:12 sits 1.5 s into V01's running timer. V02's clock on frames shared with V01-SH17 disagrees with V01's (storyboard flag 3). Fix: B05 REC 01:04:29 from 26.0 s; SH11 running from 01:04:37; V02-B01 00:06:13; and declare the V02 teaser clock. | copywriter |
| minor | V01-B11; storyboard SH18, SH21 (56-68 s) | Casting says his 'neck is set from V01 through SC18', and SC18 says 'His head keeps the set from B07'. Yet V01-B11 reads 'JEAN stays in the passenger seat, head to the door', and SH18 and SH21 are boarded 'head to the door' with nobody turning it back (storyboard flag 6). Fix: amend B11 to the post-B07 set, or write the off-screen re-turn and reconcile it with rigor. | copywriter |
| minor | D01 SC04, SC08, SC09, SC18 | Lucas asked for a decision on phone footage and that 'every shot reads as 35mm'. The camera plan's phone-video rule covers only V01, V02 and the stills. The pilot staging includes SC04's zoom through bar glass, SC08 on Jean's phone, SC09 shot entirely 'from the dash cam, its timestamp running', and SC18's front-camera screen, and none of them has a look decision. D01 also carries no look line telling execs the pilot is intended as 35mm. Fix: extend the phone rule (plus a dash-cam rule) to these scenes and give the copywriter a one-line look note for D01. | cinematographer |
| minor | V01-B07 (SH15, 45-50 s); D01 SC18 | The departments give three values for the chin turn. Casting: 'The head gives a few degrees'. Treatment: 'from east to north-east ... 45° off the door', and SC18 '45° to his own right'. Camera plan: the face finishes 'at bearing about 30°' (60° off the door), and the far-iris acceptance for the 43° yaw is built on that. Fix: lock one value and align the treatment, SC18 and the casting note to it. | director |
| minor | V01-SH09, SH15-SH16, SH21-SH24; S03 | Open production-design items. (1) PR-22 rides 'loose in the boot', but SH23 takes both jugs 'from sack 2' and SH21 carries only two bags and two sacks (flag 5). (2) PR-16 has no state before B12 (flag 7). (3) PR-01 has no side button for SH09 and no state after B08 (flag 6b). (4) The rail mirror and 110 cm rail height, and the measured seated heights, are unconfirmed, and SH15 and SH16 depend on them. (5) PR-16 needs its east-side black wrap for the SH23-SH24 silhouettes. (6) The S03 wet ring would have dried since SC05 (flag 9). (7) The PR-41 and PR-56 hexes differ from the style bible's #2A2624 and #24262B. | production_designer |
| minor | D01 SC04-SC10 (VENUS_L01) | VENUS_L01's jacket has 'No outside pockets; one inside left pocket'. The treatment puts PR-01 in her 'right jacket pocket' from SC04 to SC10, and SC05's route link depends on 'its bare black edge shows at her right jacket pocket through the whole scene'. The director's blocker to the stylist ('confirm a front pocket that holds 15 x 7.2 cm') is unanswered. Fix: add an outside right pocket sized for PR-01, or agree another placement with the director. | stylist |
| minor | D01 SC15 (about 1400-1440 s) | SND-D01-002 has 'The speaker's volume button takes three soft presses'. PR-54 has a 'physical volume ring', LOC-10's one real detail is 'the speaker's physical volume ring, which she turns', and the treatment has her 'turns the ring to about 80% (2.0)'. Fix: re-spot the level change as a ring turn. | sound_designer |
| minor | V01-SH01 to SH10 (0-34 s) | V01-SH01-VID's acceptance is 'no motion in his head and collar (freeze from frame 0 if it does)', and SH04-SH10 lock him the same way. Yet the car is open at road speed ('Wind clips the mic', SND-V01-001), and at SH15 his hair moves and 'his black cotton collar flutter[s] against his neck in the wind' (SND-V01-007). A fully frozen collar and hair reads as a pasted still and breaks continuity with B07. Fix: lock face, lids, jaw, chest and head position, allow wind flutter at the hair tips and collar edge, and write acceptance checks per region. | generation_supervisor |
| minor | V01-SH15, SH16 (45-52.4 s) | The storyboard has SH15 'PR-04 open in her right palm', and the treatment says PR-04 'stays in her right hand across the 45.0 cut and is hooked ... by 51.0'. The V01-SH15-IMG prompt leaves the glasses out. V01-SH16-IMG-V starts with them 'hooked ... at the centre of her white shirt neckline' and SH16-VID has no hook action, so the fallback is used as the primary without being declared. SND-V01-008's hinge tick at 50.5 s then plays over nothing. Fix: put the glasses in her palm in the SH15 still and generate the hook, or declare the fallback to the storyboard and sound designer. | generation_supervisor |
| minor | K07-PRINTS; V01-SH23 to SH26; S02 | The treatment asks for 'about 40 unique long-lens images ... then tile them to about 250 prints'. K07 makes 'Twelve separate candid photographs', and 'The roughly 250 wall prints are tiled from these 12', so repeats risk showing at S02's deck size. K07's only reference is CHR-JEAN-DEAD@v1 (grey-ash skin, fixed open eyes, top button fastened), while the prints must show him alive in L01. Fix: make about 40 unique prints from a living JEAN_L01 kit sheet. | generation_supervisor |
| minor | V01-SH24 to SH25 join (82.0 s) | The storyboard's method is to 'composite her silhouette pass over the SH25 clean plate, so ... the join to SH25 is exact'. The generation plan regenerates the full frame (SH24-IMG, VID1, VID2) and sets the acceptance 'After her exit, the frame matches the SH25 plate on a difference matte', which a generated full-frame clip cannot meet, so the join will pop. Fix: generate or shoot her silhouette and the moving chair as layers over the frozen SH25 plate. | generation_supervisor |
| minor | D01 SC06-SC07, SC08-SC17, SC11; V01-B11 | Continuity gaps in D01. No scene gets the two-seater back from outside the bar (director and world bible blockers). Jean's black bag (PR-05) is never planted before V01-B11. SC07's heading says LATER, yet it plays straight on from SC06's 'My turn'. SC11 has no link to the route idea (director blocker). Fix: apply the proposed fixes (a 4 s car insert in SC17, the bag on the mat in SC10, SC07 set to CONTINUOUS, and accept or rework SC11). | copywriter |

The blocker to the storyboard to 'Confirm the target platform template' is still open, and the flip icon at 63% x 70% sits near that rail. | storyboard_artist |
